// ─── Nest Track · Cloudflare Worker ─── // Handles: Anthropic API proxy + OneSignal push + ShowingTime + Email + Webhook + Inbound Leads + Weekly TC Digest + Auto-Send Offer const ALLOWED_ORIGIN = '*'; // ─── Sending domains (per-agent from-fields; reply-to is always the agent's own email) ─── // mail = transactional (status/step updates, offers, appts, billing, alerts) // news = automated nurture (Smart Plans drips + NestTracker home-value digest) // hi = email-builder broadcasts (highest complaint risk — fully isolated) // Nothing ever sends "from NestTracker" — always {AgentName} <{slug}@{domain}>. const MAIL_DOMAIN = 'mail.nesttrack.io'; // ── App download links ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Store listing URLs. Every email that offers the app picks these up automatically — set one and // the buttons, copy, and fallbacks all adjust themselves. Both stores are live as of July 2026. const APP_STORE_URL = 'https://apps.apple.com/us/app/nesttrack-crm/id6782980260'; // listed as "NestTrack CRM" const GOOGLE_PLAY_URL = 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=io.nesttrack.app'; const APP_WEB_URL = 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; const NEWS_DOMAIN = 'news.nesttrack.io'; const HI_DOMAIN = 'hi.nesttrack.io'; // Listing alerts get their own sending domain. They are the highest-volume, most automated // mail the platform sends — if they ever draw spam complaints, the damage stays here and // never touches seller reports or transaction email on the other domains. const LISTINGS_DOMAIN = 'listings.nesttrack.io'; // Build a per-agent From header: "Tom Garuccio ". // Mirrors the app's agentFromField() slug logic (first+last, a–z0–9 only) so an agent's // sending identity is identical across mail/news/hi. function _agentFromField(agent, domain){ agent = agent || {}; var first = (agent.firstName || '').trim(); var last = ''; var name = (agent.name || '').trim(); if (!first && name){ var pp = name.split(/\s+/); first = pp[0]||''; last = pp.slice(1).join(' ')||''; } else if (agent._doc){ last = (agent._doc.lastName || '').trim(); } var display = (first + ' ' + last).trim() || name || 'Your Agent'; var slug = (first + last).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''); if (!slug) slug = display.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''); if (!slug) slug = 'agent'; return display + ' <' + slug + '@' + (domain || MAIL_DOMAIN) + '>'; } // ─── Per-tenant email send cap (protects the shared Resend quota) ─── // Tune these two anytime. Daily stops a single-day blast; monthly stops slow torching. const EMAIL_DAILY_CAP = 2000; const EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP = 2000; const FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID = 'buyers-880bc'; const SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL = 'firebase-adminsdk-fbsvc@buyers-880bc.iam.gserviceaccount.com'; const FB_WEB_KEY = 'AIzaSyCy5ZkbPcGtk7I_88dVbNsX40LY-M4R_sY'; // public web key — used only to verify ID tokens server-side // ─── Firestore JWT / Token ─── async function getFirestoreToken(privateKeyPem) { const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); const header = { alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' }; const payload = { iss: SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL, sub: SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL, aud: 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', iat: now, exp: now + 3600, scope: 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/datastore' }; function b64url(obj) { return btoa(JSON.stringify(obj)).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, ''); } const signingInput = b64url(header) + '.' + b64url(payload); // Import private key const pemContents = privateKeyPem.replace(/-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----|-----END PRIVATE KEY-----|\n/g, ''); const keyData = Uint8Array.from(atob(pemContents), c => c.charCodeAt(0)); const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey( 'pkcs8', keyData.buffer, { name: 'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign'] ); const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign( 'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', cryptoKey, new TextEncoder().encode(signingInput) ); const sigBytes = new Uint8Array(signature); let sigStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < sigBytes.length; i++) sigStr += String.fromCharCode(sigBytes[i]); const jwt = signingInput + '.' + btoa(sigStr).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, ''); // Exchange JWT for access token const tokenResp = await fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: `grant_type=urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer&assertion=${jwt}` }); const tokenData = await tokenResp.json(); return tokenData.access_token; } async function firestoreWrite(token, docPath, fields) { const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}`; const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields }) }); return resp; } // ─── Read a single Firestore doc (admin) ─── async function fsGetDoc(token, docPath) { const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}`; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } }); if (!r.ok) return null; const j = await r.json(); return digDoc(j.fields || {}); } function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&').replace(//g, '>').replace(/"/g, '"'); } // ─── AES-GCM encryption for stored 3rd-party credentials ─── // Key comes from env.ENCRYPTION_KEY (base64, 32 bytes). Output: base64(iv[12] + ciphertext). async function _encKey(env){ var raw = Uint8Array.from(atob(env.ENCRYPTION_KEY), c=>c.charCodeAt(0)); return await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', raw, { name:'AES-GCM' }, false, ['encrypt','decrypt']); } async function encryptCred(env, plaintext){ if(!env.ENCRYPTION_KEY || !plaintext) return ''; var key = await _encKey(env); var iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12)); var enc = new TextEncoder().encode(plaintext); var ct = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name:'AES-GCM', iv }, key, enc)); var out = new Uint8Array(iv.length + ct.length); out.set(iv,0); out.set(ct,iv.length); var s=''; for(var i=0;ic.charCodeAt(0)); var iv = bytes.slice(0,12), ct = bytes.slice(12); var dec = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name:'AES-GCM', iv }, key, ct); return new TextDecoder().decode(dec); } catch(e){ return ''; } } // ─── List an agent's Slybroadcast audio recordings (for the VM step picker) ─── async function spListSlyAudio(creds){ if(!creds || !creds.slyEmail || !creds.slyPass) return { error:'No Slybroadcast credentials saved.' }; var form = new URLSearchParams(); form.set('c_uid', creds.slyEmail); form.set('c_password', creds.slyPass); form.set('c_method', 'get_audio_list'); try { var r = await fetch('https://www.mobile-sphere.com/gateway/vmb.aflist.php', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'}, body: form.toString() }); var t = await r.text(); if (/error|invalid|incorrect|denied|fail/i.test(t) && t.length < 120) return { error: t.slice(0,200), raw: t.slice(0,1200) }; var files = []; var isDate = function(s){ return /\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s) || /^\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{2,4}/.test(s); }; var isSystemName = function(s){ return /\.(wav|mp3|m4a|ogg)$/i.test(s) || /^[a-f0-9]{6,}$/i.test(s); }; var clean = function(s){ return String(s||'').replace(/^["'\s]+|["'\s]+$/g,'').trim(); }; // Try JSON first (array of objects, or object wrapping a list) var parsed = false; try { var j = JSON.parse(t); var arr = Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.audio_files || j.list || j.files || j.data || j.audio || []); if (Array.isArray(arr) && arr.length){ parsed = true; for (var i=0;i]+([^",}\r\n]+)/i); if (m && m[1]){ var v=clean(m[1]); if(v && !isSystemName(v) && !isDate(v)) files.push(v); return; } // delimited row: split on comma / pipe / tab / semicolon var parts = line.split(/[,|\t;]+/).map(clean).filter(Boolean); if (parts.length <= 1) return; var candidates = parts.filter(function(p){ return !isSystemName(p) && !isDate(p) && !/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}/.test(p); }); if (candidates.length){ // prefer the longest human-looking candidate candidates.sort(function(a,b){ return b.length - a.length; }); files.push(candidates[0]); } }); } // De-dupe var seen = {}, uniq = []; files.forEach(function(f){ if(f && !seen[f]){ seen[f]=1; uniq.push(f); } }); return { files: uniq, raw: t.slice(0, 1200) }; } catch(e){ return { error: e.message }; } } // ─── Native VM drop via Slybroadcast (uses agent's own creds) ─── async function spSendVM(creds, audioName, phone, callerId){ if(!creds || !creds.slyEmail) return { ok:false, reason:'no slybroadcast email saved for this agent' }; if(!creds.slyPass) return { ok:false, reason:'no slybroadcast password saved for this agent' }; if(!audioName) return { ok:false, reason:'this VM step has no audio file name' }; if(!phone) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no phone number' }; var form = new URLSearchParams(); form.set('c_uid', creds.slyEmail); form.set('c_password', creds.slyPass); form.set('c_phone', String(phone).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')); form.set('c_record_audio', audioName); form.set('c_callerID', (callerId||creds.slyCallerId||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')); form.set('c_date', 'now'); form.set('mobile_only', '1'); try { var r = await fetch('https://www.mobile-sphere.com/gateway/vmb.php', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'}, body: form.toString() }); var t = await r.text(); if (/OK|session/i.test(t)) return { ok:true, reason:'sent' }; return { ok:false, reason:'slybroadcast replied: ' + String(t).slice(0,160) }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'send error: ' + e.message }; } } // ─── Parse a single mailing-address string into thanks.io's components ─── function spParseAddress(s){ var out = { street:'', city:'', state:'', zip:'', country:'US' }; s = String(s||'').replace(/\r/g,'').replace(/\n/g, ', ').trim(); if(!s) return out; var parts = s.split(',').map(function(p){ return p.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); if (parts.length >= 3){ out.street = parts[0]; out.city = parts[1]; var tail = parts.slice(2).join(' '); var m = tail.match(/([A-Za-z]{2})\s*(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)/); if (m){ out.state = m[1].toUpperCase(); out.zip = m[2]; } else { out.state = (parts[2]||'').toUpperCase(); out.zip = parts[3]||''; } } else if (parts.length === 2){ out.street = parts[0]; var m2 = parts[1].match(/(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z]{2})\s*(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)/); if (m2){ out.city = m2[1].trim(); out.state = m2[2].toUpperCase(); out.zip = m2[3]; } else out.city = parts[1]; } else { out.street = s; } return out; } // ─── List an agent's thanks.io message templates (for the Letter step picker) ─── async function spListThanksioImageTemplates(key){ if(!key) return { error:'No thanks.io API key saved.' }; try { var r = await fetch('https://api.thanks.io/api/v2/image-templates/?items_per_page=100', { headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+key, 'Accept':'application/json' } }); if(!r.ok){ var et = await r.text(); return { error:'thanks.io '+r.status+': '+et.slice(0,150) }; } var j = await r.json(); var arr = (j && j.data) || []; var templates = arr.map(function(t){ return { id:t.id, name:t.name||('Image '+t.id), type:t.type||'' }; }); return { templates: templates }; } catch(e){ return { error:e.message }; } } async function spListThanksioTemplates(key){ if(!key) return { error:'No thanks.io API key saved.' }; try { var r = await fetch('https://api.thanks.io/api/v2/message-templates/?items_per_page=100', { headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+key, 'Accept':'application/json' } }); if(!r.ok){ var et = await r.text(); return { error:'thanks.io '+r.status+': '+et.slice(0,150) }; } var j = await r.json(); var arr = (j && j.data) || []; var templates = arr.map(function(t){ return { id:t.id, name:t.name||('Template '+t.id), type:t.type||'' }; }); // letter-type first, then the rest templates.sort(function(a,b){ return (a.type==='letter'?0:1) - (b.type==='letter'?0:1); }); return { templates: templates }; } catch(e){ return { error:e.message }; } } // ─── Text-you-send: queue a task on the agent's user doc + push them ─── function spMergeBody(body, c){ return String(body||'') .replace(/\{first\}/gi, c.firstName||'') .replace(/\{last\}/gi, c.lastName||'') .replace(/\{name\}/gi, ((c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||'')).trim()) .replace(/%FIRST_NAME%/gi, c.firstName||''); } async function spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds){ var agentUid = creds.uid || ''; if (!agentUid) return { ok:false, reason:'could not resolve the agent to notify' }; if (!c.phone) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no phone number' }; var msg = spMergeBody(step.smsBody||step.content||'', c); if (!msg.trim()) return { ok:false, reason:'this text step has no message' }; var name = ((c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||'')).trim() || 'contact'; var taskId = 'tt_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7); // Write task to users/{agentUid}/textTasks/{taskId} (covered by existing user-subcollection rules) try { var PRIVATE_KEY = env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY; var token = await getFirestoreToken(PRIVATE_KEY); var url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/users/${agentUid}/textTasks/${taskId}`; await fetch(url, { method:'PATCH', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields: { contactName: { stringValue: name }, contactId: { stringValue: c.uid || '' }, phone: { stringValue: String(c.phone) }, message: { stringValue: msg }, planName: { stringValue: (step.planName||'') }, done: { booleanValue: false }, createdAt: { integerValue: String(Date.now()) } }}) }); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'could not save text task: ' + e.message }; } // Push the agent var ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) { var _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, agentUid); await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Key ${ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e', include_aliases: { external_id: [agentUid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: ' Text ' + name }, contents: { en: msg.slice(0, 110) }, web_url: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?texts=1', data: { targetUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?texts=1' } }) }).catch(()=>{}); } return { ok:true, reason:'reminder queued + push sent' }; } // ─── Native letter via thanks.io (uses agent's own API key) ─── // opts: { templateId, message }. Sends a windowless letter. async function spSendLetter(key, opts, contact){ if(!key) return { ok:false, reason:'no thanks.io API key saved for this agent' }; opts = opts || {}; var a = spParseAddress(contact.mailingAddress||''); if(!a.street) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no mailing address' }; var recipient = { name: ((contact.firstName||'')+' '+(contact.lastName||'')).trim() || 'Resident', address: a.street, city: a.city, province: a.state, postal_code: a.zip, country: a.country }; var body = { recipients: [recipient] }; // Determine mailer type. 'postcard' needs a FRONT IMAGE (image_template_id or front_image_url) // plus a message/message_template. 'letter' (windowless) needs only a message/message_template. var mailerType = (opts.mailerType||'letter').toLowerCase(); if (mailerType === 'postcard'){ // Front image is REQUIRED for postcards. if (opts.imageTemplateId) body.image_template_id = parseInt(opts.imageTemplateId,10); else if (opts.frontImageUrl) body.front_image_url = opts.frontImageUrl; else return { ok:false, reason:'postcard needs a front image — pick an image template' }; // Back message (template or text). Postcards can technically send image-only, but we require a message. if (opts.messageTemplateId) body.message_template_id = parseInt(opts.messageTemplateId,10); else if (opts.message && opts.message.trim()) body.message = opts.message; else return { ok:false, reason:'postcard needs a message — pick a message template or write one' }; if (opts.size) body.size = opts.size; // 4x6 / 6x9 / 6x11 var pcEndpoint = 'https://api.thanks.io/api/v2/send/postcard'; try { var rp = await fetch(pcEndpoint, { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+key, 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Accept':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); if (rp.ok) return { ok:true, reason:'sent (postcard)' }; var tp = await rp.text(); return { ok:false, reason:'thanks.io '+rp.status+': '+String(tp).slice(0,160) }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'send error: '+e.message }; } } // Default: windowless letter (message only) if(!opts.templateId && !(opts.message && opts.message.trim())) return { ok:false, reason:'this letter step has no template and no message' }; if (opts.templateId) body.message_template_id = parseInt(opts.templateId,10); else body.message = opts.message; try { var r = await fetch('https://api.thanks.io/api/v2/send/windowlessletter', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+key, 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Accept':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); if (r.ok) return { ok:true, reason:'sent' }; var t = await r.text(); return { ok:false, reason:'thanks.io '+r.status+': '+String(t).slice(0,160) }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'send error: '+e.message }; } } function fsString(v) { return { stringValue: v || '' }; } function fsInt(v) { return { integerValue: String(v || 0) }; } function fsDouble(v) { return { doubleValue: Number(v) || 0 }; } function fsBool(v) { return { booleanValue: !!v }; } function fsTimestamp() { return { timestampValue: new Date().toISOString() }; } // Agent UID map — agentId short ID → Firebase UID for push notifications const AGENT_UIDS = { 'tom': 'pkVYFB12E4aTJcNlr7VhTJvETnR2', // Add other agents here as they join: 'chrissy': 'their-firebase-uid' }; // Agent email map — agentId short ID → email for lead notifications const AGENT_EMAILS = { 'tom': 'Tom@realtyincle.com', // Add other agents: 'chrissy': 'chrissy@realtyincle.com' }; // ─── Dynamic per-agent push (works for EVERY team, no hardcoded roster) ─── // Resolves any agent's Firebase uid from the publicly-written push_subscriptions/agent_{id} // doc (uid, name, teamId) that each agent's app writes on login. Falls back to the legacy // hardcoded map only as a bootstrap. This is what lets the worker notify any team member. const _OS_APP_ID = 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e'; // ── APP-ICON BADGE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // We keep our OWN badge count in Firestore (badge_counts/{uid}) and send `SetTo: ` on every // push, instead of letting OneSignal track it with `Increase: +1`. // // Why: the only way to reset OneSignal's server-side counter is a SILENT push, and Apple treats // silent pushes as best-effort — they get dropped in Low Power Mode, with Background App Refresh // off, or when the app was swiped away. So the reset almost never landed. The device badge cleared // (that part is local and instant), but OneSignal's counter kept climbing, and the next real push // resumed from the old total. One unread message would show 46. // // With SetTo, there is no counter to drift: every push carries the true number, and opening the app // writes 0 over an ordinary HTTP call that can't be silently dropped. async function _badgeNext(env, uid){ if (!uid) return 1; try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const d = await fsGetDoc(token, 'badge_counts/' + String(uid)); const n = Math.max(0, parseInt((d && d.n) || 0, 10)) + 1; await fsPatchFields(token, 'badge_counts/' + String(uid), { n: fsInt(n), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['n','updatedAt']); return n; } catch(e){ return 1; } // a counter miss should never block the push itself } // Zero the badge. Called when the app opens or returns to the foreground. async function clearBadgeForUid(env, uid){ if (!uid) return false; let wrote = false; // 1) Reset OUR counter — the authoritative one. A plain Firestore write; it always lands. try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await fsPatchFields(token, 'badge_counts/' + String(uid), { n: fsInt(0), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['n','updatedAt']); wrote = true; } catch(e){} // 2) Best-effort silent push to zero the badge already painted on the device. If iOS drops this // (it often does), it no longer matters — the next real push sends SetTo with the true count. try { const key = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (key) { await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Key ${key}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: _OS_APP_ID, include_aliases: { external_id: [String(uid)] }, target_channel: 'push', content_available: true, ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: 0 }) }); } } catch(e){} return wrote; } async function resolveAgentPush(token, agentId) { if (!agentId) return null; try { const sub = await fsGetDoc(token, 'push_subscriptions/agent_' + agentId); if (sub && sub.uid) return { uid: sub.uid, name: sub.name || '', teamId: sub.teamId || '', agentId: agentId }; } catch (e) { /* fall through to legacy bootstrap */ } if (AGENT_UIDS[agentId]) return { uid: AGENT_UIDS[agentId], name: '', teamId: '', agentId: agentId }; return null; } // Push to a single agent by agentId. Returns true if a push was attempted. async function pushToAgentId(token, env, agentId, heading, content, url) { try { const key = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (!key) return false; const who = await resolveAgentPush(token, agentId); if (!who || !who.uid) return false; const _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, who.uid); await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Key ${key}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: _OS_APP_ID, include_aliases: { external_id: [who.uid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: heading }, contents: { en: content }, web_url: url || 'https://app.nesttrack.io', data: { targetUrl: url || 'https://app.nesttrack.io' } }) }).catch(() => {}); return true; } catch (e) { console.log('pushToAgentId err:', e && e.message); return false; } } // Push to many agents (blast distribution). Returns count attempted. async function pushToAgentIds(token, env, agentIds, heading, content, url) { let n = 0; for (const aid of (agentIds || [])) { if (await pushToAgentId(token, env, aid, heading, content, url)) n++; } return n; } // ─── LEAD DISTRIBUTION ENGINE (blast / round-robin; powers auto + manual lead sharing) ─── function fsFutureTs(ms) { return { timestampValue: new Date(Date.now() + (ms || 0)).toISOString() }; } function fsStrArr(arr) { return { arrayValue: { values: (arr || []).map(function (s) { return { stringValue: String(s || '') }; }) } }; } // Partial update (does NOT wipe other fields — unlike firestoreWrite's full PATCH). async function fsPatchFields(token, docPath, fields, maskPaths) { const mask = (maskPaths || Object.keys(fields)).map(function (p) { return 'updateMask.fieldPaths=' + encodeURIComponent(p); }).join('&'); const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}?${mask}`; return await fetch(url, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields }) }); } // Read a team's lead-distribution config (written by the leader's settings UI). async function readLeadDistConfig(token, teamId) { if (!teamId) return null; try { return await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_config/leadDist_' + teamId); } catch (e) { return null; } } // Distribute a lead to a team. Creates a team_leads doc (status 'open') and fires pushes. async function distributeLead(token, env, teamId, lead, cfg) { const rotation = (cfg && Array.isArray(cfg.rotation)) ? cfg.rotation.filter(Boolean) : []; if (!rotation.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'empty rotation' }; const mode = (cfg && cfg.mode === 'roundrobin') ? 'roundrobin' : 'blast'; const leadId = 'tl_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7); const clientName = ((lead.firstName || '') + ' ' + (lead.lastName || '')).trim() || lead.email || 'New Lead'; const fields = { firstName: fsString(lead.firstName), lastName: fsString(lead.lastName), email: fsString(lead.email), phone: fsString(lead.phone), source: fsString(lead.source || 'External'), notes: fsString(lead.notes || ''), address: fsString(lead.address || ''), leadIntent: fsString(lead.intent || ''), teamId: fsString(teamId), mode: fsString(mode), status: fsString('open'), segment: fsString(lead.segment || ''), rotation: fsStrArr(rotation), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), }; let startIdx = 0; if (mode === 'roundrobin') { const last = (cfg && typeof cfg.lastIndex === 'number') ? cfg.lastIndex : -1; startIdx = (last + 1) % rotation.length; fields.currentIndex = fsInt(startIdx); fields.currentAgentId = fsString(rotation[startIdx]); fields.nextAdvanceAt = fsFutureTs(60 * 1000); } await firestoreWrite(token, 'team_leads/' + leadId, fields); const url = 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; if (mode === 'blast') { await pushToAgentIds(token, env, rotation, ' New Lead — first to claim!', clientName + ' just came in via ' + (lead.source || 'a lead source') + '. Tap to claim.', url); } else { await pushToAgentId(token, env, rotation[startIdx], ' New Lead — your turn!', clientName + ' is yours to claim for the next minute. Tap to claim.', url); // advance the team's rotation pointer so the NEXT lead starts at the next agent (fairness) await fsPatchFields(token, 'agent_config/leadDist_' + teamId, { lastIndex: fsInt(startIdx) }, ['lastIndex']); } return { ok: true, leadId, mode, count: rotation.length }; } // Advance round-robin leads whose 1-minute claim window expired. Called from the cron. async function advanceRoundRobinLeads(token, env) { let resp; try { resp = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'team_leads', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'status' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: 'open' } } } }); } catch (e) { return; } if (!Array.isArray(resp)) return; const nowMs = Date.now(); for (const r of resp) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (d.mode !== 'roundrobin') continue; const dueMs = d.nextAdvanceAt ? Date.parse(d.nextAdvanceAt) : 0; if (!dueMs || dueMs > nowMs) continue; // window hasn't lapsed const leadId = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const rotation = Array.isArray(d.rotation) ? d.rotation : []; const curIdx = (typeof d.currentIndex === 'number') ? d.currentIndex : 0; const nextIdx = curIdx + 1; const clientName = ((d.firstName || '') + ' ' + (d.lastName || '')).trim() || d.email || 'New Lead'; if (nextIdx >= rotation.length) { // Ran the whole rotation unclaimed → hand to the team's leader/owner (teamId == their agentId). await fsPatchFields(token, 'team_leads/' + leadId, { status: fsString('unclaimed'), currentAgentId: fsString('') }, ['status', 'currentAgentId']); await pushToAgentId(token, env, d.teamId, '⚠️ Lead unclaimed — needs assigning', clientName + ' went through the rotation with no claim. Assign it manually.', 'https://app.nesttrack.io'); } else { await fsPatchFields(token, 'team_leads/' + leadId, { currentIndex: fsInt(nextIdx), currentAgentId: fsString(rotation[nextIdx]), nextAdvanceAt: fsFutureTs(60 * 1000) }, ['currentIndex', 'currentAgentId', 'nextAdvanceAt']); await pushToAgentId(token, env, rotation[nextIdx], ' New Lead — your turn!', clientName + ' is yours to claim for the next minute. clean.substr(9,2) : '00'; var mi = clean.length > 11 ? clean.substr(11,2) : '00'; try { return new Date(new Date(y+'-'+mo+'-'+d+'T'+h+':'+mi+':00').getTime() + estOffsetMs).toISOString(); } catch(e) { return null; } } var summary = getVal('SUMMARY'), location = getVal('LOCATION'), dtstart = getVal('DTSTART'), dtend = getVal('DTEND'); var desc = getVal('DESCRIPTION'), uid = getVal('UID'), status = getVal('STATUS'); if (status === 'CANCELLED') return; var startISO = parseDateTime(dtstart), endISO = parseDateTime(dtend); if (startISO) { var start = new Date(startISO); if (!includeHistory && (start < todayEastern || start > cutoff)) return; } events.push({ uid: uid || ('gen-' + Math.random()), summary: summary || location || 'Showing', address: location || summary || 'Address TBD', start: startISO, end: endISO, description: desc, status }); }); events.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.start||'') < (b.start||'') ? Respects per-agent opt-out // (users/{uid}.tcDigestOff) and per-deal master switch (deal.tcEnabled). // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _digDate(s){ if(!s) return null; function digTaskStatus(deal, t, today){ var st = (deal.tcState && deal.tcState[t.key] && deal.tcState[t.key].status) || ''; if (st==='done' || st==='skip') return st; if (!t.applies(deal)) return 'skip'; var start=t.start(deal), end=t.end(deal); if (!start) return 'upcoming'; if (today < start) return 'upcoming'; if (end && today > end) return 'overdue'; return 'now'; } // Firestore REST value helpers function digVal(f){ if (!f) return undefined; const STRIPE_TRIAL_DAYS = 30; // Stripe wants form-encoded bodies with bracket notation for nested params. function _stripeEncode(params) { const parts = []; function add(key, val) { if (val === undefined || val === null) return; if (Array.isArray(val)) { val.forEach(function(v,i){ add(key+'['+i+']', v); }); } else if (typeof val === 'object') { Object.keys(val).forEach(function(k){ add(key+'['+k+']', val[k]); }); } else { parts.push(encodeURIComponent(key)+'='+encodeURIComponent(val)); } } Object.keys(params||{}).forEach(function(k){ add(k, params[k]); }); return parts.join('&'); } async function stripeAPI(env, method, path, params) { const r = await fetch('https://api.stripe.com/v1/'+path, { method: method, headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: (method === 'GET' || !params) ? undefined : _stripeEncode(params) }); return await r.json(); } // Verify a Stripe webhook signature (HMAC-SHA256 over "t.rawBody"), with 5-min replay protection. async function verifyStripeSig(rawBody, sigHeader, secret) { if (!sigHeader || !secret) return false; secret = String(secret).trim(); // strip any stray whitespace/newline picked up when the secret was pasted into env let t = null; const v1s = []; sigHeader.split(',').forEach(function(kv){ const i = kv.indexOf('='); if (i<0) return; const k = kv.slice(0,i).trim(), v = kv.slice(i+1).trim(); if (k === 't') t = v; else if (k === 'v1') v1s.push(v); }); if (!t || !v1s.length) return false; if (Math.abs(Math.floor(Date.now()/1000) - parseInt(t,10)) > 172800) return false; // 2-day window (was 300s) so dashboard Resends of older events still verify const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(secret), { name:'HMAC', hash:'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, new TextEncoder().encode(t + '.' + rawBody)); const expected = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig)).map(function(b){ return b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0'); }).join(''); return v1s.some(function(v){ if (v.length !== expected.length) return false; let diff = 0; for (let i=0;i _nowSec + (48*3600 + 600)) params.subscription_data.trial_end = _te; if (memberQty > 0) params.line_items.push({ price: memberPrice, quantity: memberQty }); Reproduces the app's // offer-summary email; CCs the listing agent; marks the offer Under Review. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function handleAutoSendOffer(request, env, corsHeaders) { const RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; const PRIVATE_KEY = env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY; if (!RESEND_API_KEY || !PRIVATE_KEY) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'missing keys' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); const { listingId, offerId } = await request.json(); if (!listingId || !offerId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'missing ids' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); const token = await getFirestoreToken(PRIVATE_KEY); const listing = await fsGetDoc(token, `offer_listings/${listingId}`); if (!listing || listing.autoSend !== true || !listing.clientUid) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'autoSend off or no client' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); } const o = await fsGetDoc(token, `offer_listings/${listingId}/offers/${offerId}`); const client = await fsGetDoc(token, `users/${listing.clientUid}`); if (!o || !client || !client.email) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'missing offer/client/email' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); } const clientFirst = client.firstName || 'there'; const rows = [ ['Offer Price', o.offerPrice], ['Financing', o.financingType], ['Down Payment', o.downPayment], ['Inspection', o.inspectionCont === 'Yes' ? '$' + o.closingCosts : null], ['Buyer Agent Comm.', o.buyerAgentComm ? o.buyerAgentComm + '%' : null], ['Submitted By', o.agentName ? (o.agentName + (o.agentBrokerage ? ' — ' + o.agentBrokerage : '')) : null], ].filter(r => r[1]).map(r => '' + esc(r[0]) + '' + '' + esc(r[1]) + '' ).join(''); const html = '
' + '

New Offer Received
' + '
' + esc(listing.address || '') + '
' + '

Hi ' + esc(clientFirst) + ',

' + '

A new offer has been submitted on your property:

' + '

' + '

' + '
'; const toArr = [client.email, client.email2].map(e => (e || '').trim()).filter((e, i, a) => e && a.indexOf(e) === i); const agentEmail = listing.agentId ? Check the app to review it.' }, web_url: 'https://app.nesttrack.io', data: { targetUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io' } }) }).catch(() => {}); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, sentTo: toArr.length }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CAMPAIGN RUNNER (Smart Plans) — walks each enrolled contact's plan, // fires due "DO" steps to the agent's Zapier webhook, advances the schedule. // Day-granular (runs daily). Waits are in days; consecutive DOs fire same pass. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function spTodayET(){ return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'America/New_York',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date()); } function spAddDaysStr(str, days){ var d = new Date((str||spTodayET())+'T12:00:00Z'); d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()+(days||0)); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); } function spWaitDays(s){ var n = parseFloat(s.amount)||1, u=s.unit||'days'; if (u==='days') return n; if (u==='hours') return n/24; if (u==='minutes') return n/1440; return n; } // Minutes-based version of a wait step (for true sub-day precision). function spWaitMinutes(s){ var n = parseFloat(s.amount)||1, u=s.unit||'days'; if (u==='minutes') return n; if (u==='hours') return n*60; if (u==='days') return n*1440; return n*1440; } // Given a total delay in minutes from now, return the next-due value. // For sub-day delays we store a full ISO timestamp (so 10-min / 2-hr waits are exact). // For day+ delays we keep the legacy YYYY-MM-DD date string (noon ET) so day-scale // nurture behaves exactly as before. function spNextDueFromMinutes(mins){ mins = Math.max(0, mins||0); if (mins < 1440){ return new Date(Date.now() + Math.round(mins*60000)).toISOString(); // full timestamp } var days = Math.round(mins/1440); return spAddDaysStr(spTodayET(), Math.max(1, days)); // legacy date string } // Is a stored smartPlanNextDue value due now? Handles BOTH formats: // full ISO timestamp ("2026-07-06T23:10:00.000Z") and legacy date string ("2026-07-06"). function spIsDue(nextDue){ if (!nextDue) return true; var s = String(nextDue); if (s.indexOf('T') !== -1){ // full timestamp → compare to now var t = Date.parse(s); return isNaN(t) ? true : (t <= Date.now()); } return s <= spTodayET(); // legacy date string → date compare } async function spSendEmail(env, step, c, plan){ var RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; if (!RESEND_API_KEY) return { ok:false, reason:'no Resend key' }; var to = (c.email||'').trim(); if (!to) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no email' }; function sub(t){ return spMergeBody(String(t==null?'':t), c); } var subject = sub(step.emailSubject||step.content||'A quick note'); var bodyHtml; if (step.emailHtml && String(step.emailHtml).trim()){ bodyHtml = sub(step.emailHtml); } else { var bodyText = sub(step.emailBody||''); var esc = bodyText.replace(/&/g,'&').replace(//g,'>').replace(/\n/g,'
'); bodyHtml = '
' + esc + '
'; } // Smart Plans = automated nurture → send as {AgentName} , reply-to the agent. // Resolve the owning agent so the from/reply are per-agent (never "from NestTracker"). var token = null, agent = null; try { token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); } catch(e){} try { if (token) agent = await _ntResolveAgent(token, c.smartPlanOwner || (plan && plan.ownerAgentId) || ''); } catch(e){} var fromField = agent ? _agentFromField(agent, NEWS_DOMAIN) : ((plan && plan.senderFrom) ? plan.senderFrom : _agentFromField({name:'Your Agent'}, NEWS_DOMAIN)); if (plan && plan.senderSignature && String(plan.senderSignature).trim()) bodyHtml = bodyHtml + plan.senderSignature; var replyTo = (agent && agent.email) ? agent.email : ((plan && plan.senderReplyTo) ? plan.senderReplyTo : ((AGENT_EMAILS[c.smartPlanOwner||'tom']) || AGENT_EMAILS['tom'] || '')); var payload = { from:fromField, to:[to], subject:subject, html:bodyHtml }; if (replyTo) payload.reply_to = replyTo; try { var resp = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+RESEND_API_KEY, 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); if (resp.ok){ const _ag = (c && c.smartPlanOwner) || (plan && plan.ownerAgentId) || ''; const _nm = ((c && c.firstName)||'') + ' ' + ((c && c.lastName)||''); if (token) _logSentEmail(token, _ag, to, subject, 'smart-plan', _nm.trim(), (c && c.uid) || ''); return { ok:true, reason:'email sent' }; } var t = await resp.text(); return { ok:false, reason:'resend '+resp.status+': '+String(t).slice(0,100) }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'email error: '+e.message }; } } // Auto-enroll a contact in NestTracker from a Smart Plan step. Reuses the exact same // enrollment endpoint as the manual "Add to NestTracker" (so ATTOM valuation, cap check, // anti-spam address gate, and billing stay identical). Auto path = live enroll, no preview. async function spEnrollNestTrackerAuto(c, plan, env, token, step){ step = step || {}; var owner = c.smartPlanOwner || (plan && plan.ownerAgentId) || ''; if (!owner) return { ok:false, reason:'no owner agent — cannot enroll' }; if (!c.uid) return { ok:false, reason:'no contact id' }; var addr = (c.mailingAddress || c.address || '').trim(); if (!addr) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no mailing address — skipped' }; try { var tok = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); // Dedup: already enrolled? (a nesttracker_homes doc for this contact + active) try { var existing = await digRunQuery(tok, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ compositeFilter:{ op:'AND', filters:[ { fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'contactId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:c.uid} } }, { fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'active'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{booleanValue:true} } } ]}} }); if (Array.isArray(existing) && existing.some(function(r){return r.document;})) { return { ok:true, reason:'already in NestTracker — skipped' }; } } catch(e){} // Reuse the real enrollment endpoint (handles ATTOM valuation, cap, anti-spam address gate). var _pp = c.purchasePrice || c.salePrice || ''; var _pd = c.purchaseDate || c.closingDate || ''; var fakeReq = { json: async function(){ return { contact: { uid: c.uid, firstName: c.firstName||'', lastName: c.lastName||'', email: c.email||'', mailingAddress: addr, purchasePrice: _pp, purchaseDate: _pd, // Never, and not configurable. A plan runs on whoever enters the segment — usually a cold // lead who stopped responding. They get the quarterly value email and nothing else. The home // dashboard is a relationship tool and only ever switches on from the client card. setupHome: false } }; } }; var resp = await apiNestTrackerEnroll(fakeReq, env, { 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, { agentId: owner }); var data = {}; try { data = await resp.json(); } catch(e){} if (!data.ok){ // Surface the real skip reason (cap reached, needs agent address, bad address, etc.) return { ok:false, reason: 'NestTracker: ' + (data.reason || 'could not enroll') }; } // A NEW enrollment comes back PENDING (active:false). Decide what to do without an agent watching: // • Confident value → auto-confirm (flip live) so it sends. Silent, no push. // • No value OR low confidence → leave pending, flag needsReview, and push the agent to review. var noValue = data.needsManualValue || !data.value || Number(data.value) <= 0; var lowConf = !!data.lowConfidence; if (noValue || lowConf){ var reviewReason = noValue ? 'No automated value — enter the home value to finish enrolling.' : 'Low-confidence estimate — review the value before it sends.'; // Flag the home so the app shows a "Needs review" badge in the NestTracker list. if (data.token){ try { await fsPatchFields(tok, 'nesttracker_homes/'+data.token, { needsReview: fsBool(true), reviewReason: fsString(reviewReason) }, ['needsReview','reviewReason']); } catch(e){} } // Push the owning agent (same push path used elsewhere). var who2 = ((c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||'')).trim() || 'a client'; try { await pushToAgentId(tok, env, owner, ' A home value needs your review', 'Tap to confirm '+who2+'\u2019s NestTracker report before it sends.', 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?ntdash=1'); } catch(e){} return { ok:true, reason:'enrolled — flagged for review ('+(noValue?'no value':'low confidence')+')' }; } // Confident value → auto-confirm live. if (data.token){ var confReq = { json: async function(){ return { token: data.token }; } }; try { var cResp = await apiNestTrackerConfirm(confReq, env, { 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, { agentId: owner }); var cData = {}; try { cData = await cResp.json(); } catch(e){} if (cData && cData.ok) return { ok:true, reason:'enrolled + live in NestTracker' }; return { ok:false, reason:'NestTracker: enrolled but could not activate — '+((cData&&cData.reason)||'left pending') }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'NestTracker: enrolled but activation failed — '+e.message }; } } return { ok:true, reason:'enrolled in NestTracker' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'NestTracker enroll error: '+e.message }; } } async function spFireStep(c, plan, step, env, creds, token){ var ch = step.channel || 'zapier'; // Native channels — send directly via the agent's own account if (ch === 'email'){ return await spSendEmail(env, step, c, plan); } if (ch === 'vm'){ return await spSendVM(creds||{}, step.audioName||step.content||'', c.phone||'', step.callerId||''); } if (ch === 'letter'){ return await spSendLetter((creds||{}).thanksioKey||'', { mailerType: step.mailerType||'letter', templateId: step.letterTemplateId||'', message: step.letterBody||step.content||'', imageTemplateId: step.imageTemplateId||'', frontImageUrl: step.frontImageUrl||'', messageTemplateId: step.messageTemplateId||step.letterTemplateId||'', size: step.postcardSize||'4x6' }, c); } if (ch === 'texttap'){ return await spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds||{}); } // ── State-change action steps (change the contact, don't send a touch) ── if (ch === 'setstatus'){ var newStatus = (step.newStatus||'').trim(); var okStatuses = ['pipeline','active','nurture','cold','past','contact']; if (okStatuses.indexOf(newStatus) < 0) return { ok:false, reason:'invalid status "'+newStatus+'"' }; if (!c.uid) return { ok:false, reason:'no contact id' }; try { var tokS = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await fsPatchFields(tokS, 'users/'+c.uid, { crmStatus: fsString(newStatus) }, ['crmStatus']); return { ok:true, reason:'status → '+newStatus }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'status change error: '+e.message }; } } if (ch === 'setsegment'){ var newSeg = (step.newSegment||'').trim(); if (!c.uid) return { ok:false, reason:'no contact id' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'segment change error: '+e.message }; } } if (ch === 'nesttracker'){ return await spEnrollNestTrackerAuto(c, plan, env, token, step); } // Zapier channels (text / generic) — fire the step's own webhook URL if set, // OTHERWISE log a smart-plan event for the native Zapier polling trigger. var webhookUrl = step.webhookUrl || ''; if (!webhookUrl) { // Native Zapier path: log an event Zapier can poll (replaces pasted webhooks). try { var _tok = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); var _logged = await logSmartPlanEvent(_tok, c, plan, step); return _logged ? { ok:true, reason:'zapier event logged' } : { ok:false, reason:'could not log zapier event' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'zapier event error: ' + e.message }; } } var payload = { event: 'smartplan_step', channel: ch, label: step.content || '', content: step.content || '', planName: plan.name || '', firstName: c.firstName || '', lastName: c.lastName || '', email: c.email || '', phone: c.phone || '', mailingAddress: c.mailingAddress || '', contactId: c.uid || '', agentId: c.smartPlanOwner || '' }; try { await fetch(webhookUrl, { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); return { ok:true, reason:'webhook fired' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'webhook error: ' + e.message }; } } async function spWriteProgress(token, uid, c){ var url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/users/${uid}` + `?updateMask.fieldPaths=smartPlanStatus&updateMask.fieldPaths=smartPlanStep&updateMask.fieldPaths=smartPlanNextDue`; await fetch(url, { method:'PATCH', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields: { smartPlanStatus: { stringValue: c.smartPlanStatus||'active' }, smartPlanStep: { integerValue: String(c.smartPlanStep||0) }, smartPlanNextDue:{ stringValue: c.smartPlanNextDue||spTodayET() } }}) }).catch(()=>{}); } async function spProcessContact(token, c, plan, today, env, creds, fireLog){ var steps = plan.steps || []; var idx = parseInt(c.smartPlanStep,10) || 0; var fired = 0; // Fire consecutive DO steps (no wait between them) in this pass while (idx < steps.length && steps[idx] && steps[idx].kind === 'do'){ var res = await spFireStep(c, plan, steps[idx], env, creds, token); if (res && res.ok) fired++; if (fireLog) fireLog.push({ contact:(c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||''), channel:steps[idx].channel||'', ok:!!(res&&res.ok), reason:(res&&res.reason)||'unknown' }); idx++; } // Now at a wait or end if (idx >= steps.length){ c.smartPlanStatus = 'completed'; c.smartPlanStep = idx; } else { // consume consecutive waits to compute next due time (real minutes, not day-rounded) var delayMin = 0; while (idx < steps.length && steps[idx] && steps[idx].kind === 'wait'){ delayMin += spWaitMinutes(steps[idx]); idx++; } if (idx >= steps.length){ c.smartPlanStatus = 'completed'; c.smartPlanStep = idx; } else { c.smartPlanNextDue = spNextDueFromMinutes(delayMin); c.smartPlanStep = idx; } } await spWriteProgress(token, c.uid, c); return fired; } async function campaignRunner(token, env){ var today = spTodayET(); var diag = { today: today, activeFound: 0, noPlanId: 0, notDue: 0, planMissing: 0, dueProcessed: 0, sample: [], fires: [] }; // 1. active enrollments var resp = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'users', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'smartPlanStatus'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:'active'} } } }); if (!Array.isArray(resp)) return { processed:0, fired:0, diag: { error:'active-query returned non-array (query failed or index missing)' } }; diag.activeFound = resp.length; // Build per-agent credential map (agentId -> slybroadcast/thanks.io creds) var agentResp = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'users', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'role'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:'agent'} } } }); var credsMap = {}; if (Array.isArray(agentResp)) for (const ar of agentResp){ if (!ar.document || !ar.document.fields) continue; var au = digDoc(ar.document.fields); if (!au.agentId) continue; credsMap[au.agentId] = { uid: ar.document.name.split('/').pop(), slyEmail: au.slyEmail||'', slyCallerId: au.slyCallerId||'', slyPass: au.slyPass || '', thanksioKey: au.thanksioKey || '' }; } // process due contacts (native VM/letter use agent creds; text/zapier use per-step webhook) var planCache = {}; var processed = 0, fired = 0; for (const r of resp){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; var c = digDoc(r.document.fields); c.uid = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); if (diag.sample.length < 5) diag.sample.push({ name:(c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||''), plan:c.smartPlanId||'(none)', status:c.smartPlanStatus||'', due:c.smartPlanNextDue||'', owner:c.smartPlanOwner||'', step:c.smartPlanStep }); if (!c.smartPlanId || c.smartPlanStatus !== 'active'){ diag.noPlanId++; continue; } if (!spIsDue(c.smartPlanNextDue)){ diag.notDue++; continue; } // not due yet var owner = c.smartPlanOwner; if (!owner) { continue; } // no owner → skip; never default to a person var key = owner + '/' + c.smartPlanId; var plan = planCache[key]; if (plan === undefined){ plan = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agents/'+owner+'/smartPlans/'+c.smartPlanId); planCache[key] = plan; } if (!plan || !plan.steps){ diag.planMissing++; continue; } // plan deleted → leave enrollment as-is diag.dueProcessed++; fired += await spProcessContact(token, c, plan, today, env, credsMap[owner]||{}, diag.fires); processed++; if (processed >= 40) break; // per-run cap — stay under the Worker subrequest limit; the rest roll to the next cron tick (runs every minute) } return { processed, fired, diag }; } async function handleWeeklyDigest(env, token, diagnostic){ const RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; const _diag = { agents:0, agentsWithEmail:0, deals:0, activeDeals:0, sent:0, sentTo:[], skipped:[], activeList:[] }; if (!RESEND_API_KEY) { if(diagnostic) return Object.assign(_diag,{error:'no RESEND_API_KEY'}); return; } const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0); // 1) All agent users → map agentId -> { email, optOut, name } const agentsResp = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId:'users', allDescendants:false }], where: { fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'role'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:'agent'} } } }); const agentMap = {}; for (const r of agentsResp){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const u = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (!u.agentId) continue; agentMap[u.agentId] = { email: u.email||'', optOut: u.tcDigestOff===true, name: ((u.firstName||'')+' '+(u.lastName||'')).trim() || u.agentId }; _diag.agents++; if (u.email) _diag.agentsWithEmail++; } // 2) All deals (collection group) → group by agentId const dealsResp = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'deals', allDescendants:true }] }); const byAgent = {}; for (const r of dealsResp){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); _diag.deals++; if (d.tcEnabled === false) continue; if (d.isPracticeDeal) continue; // onboarding sample data — never a real transaction if (!d.contractDate) continue; // only under-contract deals have tasks // A deal can have a contractDate but have since fallen back to market (step dropped). Require the // CURRENT step to still be in the under-contract range — not just that a contractDate once existed. const _step = (d.step != null) ? d.step : 0; const _isSeller = d.dealType === 'seller'; const underContract = _isSeller ? (_step >= 9 && _step < 13) : (_step >= 5 && _step < 11); if (!underContract) continue; // back on market / pipeline / closed → no tasks const aid = d.agentId; if (!aid) continue; // no owner → do nothing. NEVER fall back to a person. (byAgent[aid] = byAgent[aid] || []).push(d); if (diagnostic) _diag.activeList.push({ addr: d.address || d.clientName || '(no address)', agentId: aid, step: _step, type: d.dealType||'buyer', contractDate: d.contractDate||'', path: (r.document.name||'').split('/documents/').pop(), clientUid: d.clientUid||'' }); _diag.activeDeals++; } // 3) Build + send each agent's digest for (const aid in byAgent){ const agent = agentMap[aid]; if (!agent || !agent.email || agent.optOut) { _diag.skipped.push(aid + (!agent?' (no agent record)':(!agent.email?' (no email)':' (opted out)'))); continue; } const blocks = []; for (const deal of byAgent[aid]){ const overdue=[], now=[], soon=[]; for (const t of DIGEST_TASKS){ const s = digTaskStatus(deal, t, today); if (s==='overdue') overdue.push(t.label); else if (s==='now') now.push(t.label); else if (s==='upcoming'){ const st=t.start(deal); if (st && (st-today)/86400000 <= 7) soon.push(t.label); } } if (!overdue.length && !now.length && !soon.length) continue; const addr = deal.address || (deal.clientName||'Deal'); const li = (arr,color)=> arr.length ? `
${arr.map(x=>'• '+x).join('
')}
` : ''; blocks.push(`
${addr}
${ overdue.length ? `
Overdue
`+li(overdue,'#B91C1C') : '' } ${ now.length ? `
Do now
`+li(now,'#065F46') : '' } ${ soon.length ? `
This week
`+li(soon,'#6B7280') : '' }
`); } if (!blocks.length) { _diag.skipped.push(aid + ' (no actionable tasks)'); continue; } // nothing actionable → don't send a blank digest const html = `
🤖
Your Week — Transaction Coordinator
${today.toLocaleDateString('en-US',{weekday:'long',month:'long',day:'numeric'})}
${blocks.join('')} Open Nest Track →
Turn this off in your Agent Profile.
`; const _sendResp = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`, 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ from:'Nest Track ', to:[agent.email], subject:` Your week — ${blocks.length} deal${blocks.length===1?'':'s'} need attention`, html }) }).catch((e)=>{ _diag.skipped.push(aid+' (send error: '+(e&&e.message||'?')+')'); return null; }); if (_sendResp) { _diag.sent++; _diag.sentTo.push(agent.email); } } if (diagnostic) return _diag; } // ─── Scheduled Cron Handler — fires every minute ─── // ── Onboarding email drip ────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Sends the 9-email welcome sequence to self-serve agents. Cadence: day 0,3,6,9, // 12,15,18,21,24 from trialStartedAt. Idempotent: each user doc tracks obeSent[] // so an email is never sent twice. Skip if neither has a body. const ov = items[due.key] || {}; const fb2 = FALLBACK[due.key] || {}; const subject = (ov.subject && ov.subject.trim()) || fb2.subject || ''; const body = (ov.body && ov.body.trim()) || fb2.body || ''; const video = (ov.video && ov.video.trim()) || ''; if(!subject || !body) continue; // Build the branded email (agent's own from-address + optional video block). const agentName = ((f.firstName&&f.firstName.stringValue)||'') + ' ' + ((f.lastName&&f.lastName.stringValue)||''); const fromField = _agentFromField({ firstName:(f.firstName&&f.firstName.stringValue)||'', name:agentName.trim() }, MAIL_DOMAIN); const videoBlock = video ? '' : '

\u2014 Tom"} }; // ── Maintenance reminders ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Once a day: find homes with a task due, push the homeowner one task. Never a digest, never a // daily nag — if they ignore it the task sits overdue in the app and we nudge again a fortnight // later. The push carries the filter size when we know it, because "change your furnace filter · // 16x25x1" is something you can go do, and "change your furnace filter" is something you look up // later and forget. That difference is the whole product. var MAINT_PUSH_CAP = 40; // stay under the Worker subrequest limit; the rest catch tomorrow // OneSignal is keyed on the FIREBASE AUTH uid \u2014 the app calls OneSignal.login(currentUser.uid). // But callers here pass a client DOCUMENT id, and for agent-created contacts those differ: // the doc is contact_1779137702533_gnko5 while the auth uid is stored on the record as // portalUid. Optional // token: without it, or if the lookup fails, behaviour is exactly what it was before. async function _pushResolveUid(token, uid) { if (!token || !uid) return uid; try { const d = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + uid); if (d && d.portalUid && String(d.portalUid).trim()) return String(d.portalUid).trim(); } catch (e) {} return uid; } async function _pushClient(env, uid, heading, content, opts){ const key = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (!key || !uid) return false; opts = opts || {}; try { if (opts.token) uid = await _pushResolveUid(opts.token, uid); const url = opts.url || 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; const _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, uid); const r = await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization': `Key ${key}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e', include_aliases: { external_id: [uid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: heading }, contents: { en: content }, web_url: url, data: { targetUrl: url } }) }); return !!(r && r.ok); } catch(e){ return false; } } // Which tasks earn an email. The renewal date is inferred from the closing date, so this reads // as a heads-up to go check rather than a claim about their actual policy. insurance: 'your home insurance should be coming up for renewal in about a month. Worth a look at the rate before it auto-renews.', home_anniversary: 'hard to believe how fast it goes \u2014 happy anniversary in the house.' }; var line = LINES[d.key] || ('quick reminder \u2014 it\u2019s about time to ' + esc(String(d.title||'').replace(/^Have your /,'').replace(/^Flush your /,'flush your ').toLowerCase()) + '.'); // Agent's own opening line from the editor overrides the default. It already includes their // greeting, so it becomes the whole top line rather than getting a second "Hey" prepended. var opener = d.customBody ? esc(d.customBody) : (hi + ' ' + line); var pros=''; if (d.key === 'home_anniversary'){ pros = ''; // an anniversary note does not come with a list of contractors } else if (d.partners && d.partners.length){ pros = '

'); var unsub = 'turn off these reminders'; // Same reason as the app's wrapper: no declared charset means the client guesses, and every // em dash and curly quote in this email arrives as mojibake. return '' + '' + '

} catch(e){ console.log('maintenance query:', e && e.message); return { pushed:0 }; } let pushed = 0, skipped = 0, emailed = 0, realigned = 0; for (const r of (rows||[])){ if (pushed >= MAINT_PUSH_CAP) break; continue; } let tasks = []; try { tasks = JSON.parse(h.tasksJson); } catch(e){ continue; } // Fires ONCE per occurrence, and only if it is actually current. A task more than a few // days past due is stale \u2014 the moment for it has gone \u2014 so it is silently re-anchored to // its next real date instead. Nothing about a spring reminder is useful in July, and a // client who gets one thinks their agent is asleep at the wheel. const STALE_AFTER_DAYS = 3; const due = tasks.filter(function(t){ if (t.enabled === false || !t.nextDue) return false; return t.nextDue <= today; }); if (!due.length){ skipped++; continue; } const t = due[0]; // one task per home per run const tok = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); // How late is it? A missed cron run should still send; a missed season should not. let _lateDays = 0; const _dueMs2 = Date.parse(String(t.nextDue) + 'T12:00:00Z'); if (!isNaN(_dueMs2)) _lateDays = Math.floor((Date.parse(today + 'T12:00:00Z') - _dueMs2) / 86400000); const _stale = _lateDays > STALE_AFTER_DAYS; if (!_stale){ const body = (t.spec ? t.spec + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + 'Tap to mark it done.'; // Push and email are independent. A device with no push token must not cost the client // their email, which is what happened when a failed push skipped the rest of the block. // Pass the token so the auth uid can be resolved \u2014 without it these reached nobody // for any client whose document id differs from their auth uid. try { await _pushClient(env, h.contactUid, t.title, body, { token: token }); } catch(e){} // Big-ticket tasks (HVAC, gutters, water heater, insurance) also email \u2014 with the agent's // pro attached \u2014 to reach the client whether or not they ever open the app. if (MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS[t.key] && h.email && h.maintEmailsOff !== true){ try { const agent = await _ntResolveAgent(token, h.agentId); } else { const _every = Math.max(1, Number(t.everyDays) || 90); let _nd = Date.parse(String(t.nextDue) + 'T12:00:00Z'); if (isNaN(_nd)) _nd = Date.now(); do { _nd += _every*86400000; } while (_ymd(_nd) <= today);
