return { pushed, skipped, emailed, realigned }; } async function handleScheduled(env) { const ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; const ONESIGNAL_APP_ID = 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e'; const PRIVATE_KEY = env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY; if (!PRIVATE_KEY || !ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) return; try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(PRIVATE_KEY); // ── Round-robin lead distribution: advance any leads whose 1-minute claim window lapsed ── try { await advanceRoundRobinLeads(token, env); } catch (e) { console.log('advanceRoundRobinLeads:', e && e.message); } // ── Age out old listing cards — once a day at 4am ET, when nobody is looking ── { const _p = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/New_York', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date()); const _h = parseInt((_p.find(p => p.type === 'hour') || {}).value, 10); const _m = parseInt((_p.find(p => p.type === 'minute') || {}).value, 10); if (_h === 4 && _m === 0) { try { const r = await _mlsExpireListings(env, token, false); console.log('mls expiry:', r.ok ? (r.expired + ' expired, ' + r.kept + ' kept, ' + r.scanned + ' scanned') : ('failed: ' + r.reason)); } catch (e) { console.log('mls expiry:', e && e.message); } } } // ── Weekly TC digest — Mondays at 8:00 AM Eastern, year-round (DST-proof) ── // Pinned to America/New_York so it stays 8am whether it's EDT or EST — set once, never touch. { const _et = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/New_York', weekday: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date()); const _wd = (_et.find(p => p.type === 'weekday') || {}).value; const _hr = parseInt((_et.find(p => p.type === 'hour') || {}).value, 10); const _mn = parseInt((_et.find(p => p.type === 'minute') || {}).value, 10); if (_wd === 'Mon' && _hr === 8 && _mn === 0) { try { await handleWeeklyDigest(env, token); } catch(e){ console.log('digest error:', e.message); } } } // ── Campaign runner — top of each hour, 8 AM–8 PM ET (quiet overnight). ── // (Agents can fire a contact's plan instantly with the "Fire now" button.) { const _ct = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/New_York', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date()); const _chr = parseInt((_ct.find(p => p.type === 'hour') || {}).value, 10); const _cmn = parseInt((_ct.find(p => p.type === 'minute') || {}).value, 10); // Run every cron tick during business hours (8 AM–8 PM ET), not just the top of the hour, // so sub-hour waits (e.g. "text 10 min after the VM drop") fire close to on time. // Actual granularity is bounded by the Cloudflare cron interval (set to every few minutes // in wrangler cron triggers). Day-scale steps are unaffected — they're only due once/day anyway. if (_chr >= 8 && _chr <= 20) { try { await campaignRunner(token, env); } catch(e){ console.log('campaign runner error:', e.message); } } } // ── NestTracker quarterly drift + teaser (anniversary-based; self-gates via lastPulledAt). ── // Runs top-of-hour, 9 AM–6 PM ET so sends land in business hours. Each run processes up to // 25 due homes to pace ATTOM/Resend; the rest roll to the next hourly run. { const _q = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone:'America/New_York', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', hour12:false }).formatToParts(new Date()); const _qhr = parseInt((_q.find(p=>p.type==='hour')||{}).value,10); const _qmn = parseInt((_q.find(p=>p.type==='minute')||{}).value,10); if (_qmn === 0 && _qhr >= 9 && _qhr <= 18) { try { await nesttrackerQuarterlySweep(token, env, { cap: NESTTRACKER_SWEEP_CAP }); } catch(e){ console.log('nesttracker sweep error:', e.message); } } } // ── Onboarding email drip — once daily at 10 AM ET. Sends the 9-email welcome // sequence to self-serve agents based on trial age (day 0,3,6…24). Tracks sent // emails per user so nothing repeats. ── { const _o = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone:'America/New_York', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', hour12:false }).formatToParts(new Date()); const _ohr = parseInt((_o.find(p=>p.type==='hour')||{}).value,10); const _omn = parseInt((_o.find(p=>p.type==='minute')||{}).value,10); if (_omn === 0 && _ohr === 10) { try { await onboardingEmailDrip(token, env); } catch(e){ console.log('onboarding drip error:', e && e.message); } } // ── Home maintenance reminders — once daily at 9 AM ET, before the workday swallows them. ── if (_omn === 0 && _ohr === 9) { try { await maintenanceReminders(token, env); } catch(e){ console.log('maintenance reminder error:', e && e.message); } } } const now = new Date(); // Format to YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM for comparison const nowStr = now.getFullYear()+'-' +String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-' +String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'T' +String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':' +String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'); // Query all agents for unfired reminders // We use a collection group query via REST const queryUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents:runQuery`; const queryBody = { structuredQuery: { from: [{ collectionId: 'todoReminders', allDescendants: true }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'fired' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: false } } } } }; const queryResp = await fetch(queryUrl, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(queryBody) }); const results = await queryResp.json(); for (const result of results) { if (!result.document) continue; const fields = result.document.fields; if (!fields) continue; const reminderTime = fields.reminderTime?.stringValue || ''; const uid = fields.uid?.stringValue || ''; const text = fields.text?.stringValue || 'Reminder'; const docName = result.document.name; // Check if this reminder is due (matches current minute) if (reminderTime.substring(0, 16) !== nowStr) continue; // Fire push const _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, uid); await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Key ${ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: ONESIGNAL_APP_ID, include_aliases: { external_id: [uid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: ' To-Do Reminder' }, contents: { en: text }, web_url: 'https://app.nesttrack.io', data: { targetUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io' } }) }).catch(() => {}); // Mark as fired await fetch(`https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/${docName}?updateMask.fieldPaths=fired`, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields: { fired: { booleanValue: true } } }) }).catch(() => {}); } } catch(e) { console.log('Scheduled reminder error:', e.message); } // Clean up team chat messages older than 60 days try { const RTDB_URL = 'https://buyers-880bc-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com'; const cutoff = Date.now() - (60 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); // Get all messages const rtdbResp = await fetch(`${RTDB_URL}/chats/team_chat/messages.json?auth=${env.FIREBASE_RTDB_SECRET||''}`); if (rtdbResp.ok) { const messages = await rtdbResp.json(); if (messages) { const toDelete = Object.entries(messages).filter(([k,v]) => v && v.ts && v.ts < cutoff); for (const [key] of toDelete) { await fetch(`${RTDB_URL}/chats/team_chat/messages/${key}.json?auth=${env.FIREBASE_RTDB_SECRET||''}`, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch(() => {}); } if (toDelete.length > 0) console.log(`Deleted ${toDelete.length} old team chat messages`); } } } catch(e) { console.log('Chat cleanup error:', e.message); } } // ── Free, code-only lead-email parser (no API cost). Pulls name/email/phone/address out of a // forwarded lead email across common formats. Whatever it can't cleanly find is left blank; // the full readable text is kept in notes so the agent never loses context. ── function _decodeEmailBody(raw) { let s = String(raw || ''); // If a base64 body part is present, decode the first one. const b64m = s.match(/Content-Transfer-Encoding:\s*base64[\s\S]*?\r?\n\r?\n([A-Za-z0-9+/=\r\n]{40,})/i); if (b64m) { try { s = (typeof atob === 'function') ? atob(b64m[1].replace(/\s+/g, '')) : s; } catch (e) {} } // Decode quoted-printable (=\n soft breaks and =XX hex). s = s.replace(/=\r?\n/g, '').replace(/=([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})/g, function (_, h) { try { return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)); } catch (e) { return _; } }); // HTML → text: keep line breaks, drop tags, decode a few entities. s = s.replace(/<\s*br\s*\/?>/gi, '\n').replace(/<\/(p|div|tr|li|h[1-6]|table)>/gi, '\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' '); s = s.replace(/ /gi, ' ').replace(/&/gi, '&').replace(/</gi, '<').replace(/>/gi, '>').replace(/'/g, "'").replace(/’/gi, "'").replace(/"/gi, '"'); return s; } // ── LIST-EMAIL INGEST (CSV link in the body) ──────────────────────────────── // Some lead sources (Landvoice, etc.) don't put the lead in the email — they send a link to a CSV // holding the whole day's list. Detect that link, fetch it, and import every row instead of trying // to parse one lead out of the body. Auto-detected: no link → normal single-lead path. // Pull the FIRST CSV download link out of an email body. Landvoice ships the same URL twice (one // per MIME part, with slightly different query styles), so we take one and ignore the rest. function _findListCsvUrl(raw){ if (!raw) return ''; const s = String(raw); // Landvoice: /api/{fsbo|expired|preforeclosure|...}/listing/csv?...&tok=... const m = s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]*\/api\/[a-z0-9_-]+\/listing\/csv\?[^\s"'<>]+/i); if (m && m[0]) return m[0].replace(/&/gi, '&').replace(/[.,;)\]]+$/, ''); return ''; } // RFC4180-ish CSV parser: handles quoted fields, embedded commas, doubled quotes, CRLF. function _parseCsv(text){ const rows = []; let row = [], field = '', inQ = false; const s = String(text || ''); for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++){ const c = s[i]; if (inQ){ if (c === '"'){ if (s[i+1] === '"'){ field += '"'; i++; } else inQ = false; } else field += c; } else { if (c === '"') inQ = true; else if (c === ','){ row.push(field); field = ''; } else if (c === '\r'){ /* skip */ } else if (c === '\n'){ row.push(field); rows.push(row); row = []; field = ''; } else field += c; } } if (field !== '' || row.length){ row.push(field); rows.push(row); } return rows.filter(function(r){ return r.length && r.some(function(v){ return String(v).trim() !== ''; }); }); } // Map one CSV row (Landvoice column names) → the lead shape the inbound pipeline already speaks. // Returns null for rows we deliberately skip (already sold). function _mapListRow(get){ const status = (get('Status from MLS') || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (status === 'sold' || status === 'closed') return null; // never solicit someone who already sold const street = (get('Property Street') || '').trim(); const city = (get('Property City') || '').trim(); const st = (get('Property State') || '').trim(); const zip = (get('Property Zip') || '').trim(); const addr = [street, [city, st].filter(Boolean).join(', '), zip].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim(); // Landvoice often has no seller name. Fall back to the street so the list is readable. let firstName = '', lastName = ''; const seller = (get('Seller') || '').trim(); if (seller){ const p = seller.split(/\s+/); firstName = p.shift() || ''; lastName = p.join(' '); } else { firstName = street || 'FSBO Lead'; } const phone = (get('Phone 1') || get('Phone 2') || '').trim(); const price = (get('Price') || '').trim(); const beds = (get('Bedrooms') || '').trim(); const sqft = (get('Square Feet') || '').trim(); const adTxt = (get('Ad Text') || '').trim(); const dnc = (get('Phone 1 Do Not Call') || '').trim(); // Ad Text already states price/beds/sqft. The only thing worth appending is a DNC flag, which is // the one field the agent genuinely needs surfaced before they pick up the phone. const notes = [adTxt.replace(/\$\$+/g, '$'), dnc ? ('⚠️ DO NOT CALL flag from source: ' + dnc) : ''].filter(Boolean).join('\n'); return { externalId: (get('LandvoiceID') || '').trim(), // stable dedupe key across daily sends firstName: firstName, lastName: lastName, email: (get('EMail') || '').trim(), phone: phone, address: addr, notes: notes, leadType: 'seller', // FSBO/expired sellers are seller-side leads dnc: !!dnc }; } function parseLeadEmail(raw, excludeEmail) { if (!raw) return null; const text = _decodeEmailBody(raw); const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).map(function (l) { return l.replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ').trim(); }).filter(function (l) { return l.length; }); const flat = lines.join('\n'); const out = { firstName: '', lastName: '', email: '', phone: '', address: '', leadType: '', source: '', notes: '' }; const label = function (re) { const m = flat.match(re); return m ? String(m[1]).trim() : ''; }; // EMAIL — first real-looking address that isn't the platform's own no-reply. const _excl = String(excludeEmail || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const emails = flat.match(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi) || []; const badEmail = /(no-?reply|noreply|notification|do-?not-?reply|mailer|@zillow|@clever|@realtor|@homelight|@trulia|@redfin|@mail\.|resend\.app)/i; out.email = (emails.find(function (e) { return !badEmail.test(e) && e.toLowerCase() !== _excl; }) || '').trim(); // PHONE — labeled first, else first phone-shaped token; validate 10-11 digits. let phone = label(/Phone(?:\s*Number)?\s*[:\-]\s*([+()0-9.\-\s]{7,}\d)/i); if (!phone) { const pm = flat.match(/(\+?\d[\d().\-\s]{8,}\d)/); phone = pm ? pm[1] : ''; } let pdig = phone.replace(/\D/g, ''); if (pdig.length === 11 && pdig[0] === '1') pdig = pdig.slice(1); out.phone = (pdig.length === 10) ? ('(' + pdig.slice(0, 3) + ') ' + pdig.slice(3, 6) + '-' + pdig.slice(6)) : ''; // ADDRESS / LEAD TYPE — labeled. out.address = label(/Address\s*[:\-]\s*(.+)/i); const lt = label(/Lead\s*Type[^:\-]*[:\-]\s*(buyer|seller|both)/i).toLowerCase(); out.leadType = (lt === 'buyer' || lt === 'seller' || lt === 'both') ? lt : ''; // NAME — several strategies, easy → hard. let name = label(/^([A-Z][A-Za-z.'\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z.'\-]+){0,2})'s\s+Info/im); // "Anthony Iskandar's Info" if (!name) name = label(/\bName\s*[:\-]\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z.'\- ]{1,50})/i); // "Name: ..." if (!name) name = label(/Lead\s*(?:Receipt|for)\s*[:\-]\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z.'\- ]{1,50})/i); // "Lead Receipt: ..." if (!name) { // "new connection from..." → next name-like line for (let i = 0; i < lines.length && !name; i++) { if (/new (connection|lead|contact)/i.test(lines[i]) || /premier agent/i.test(lines[i])) { for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(i + 7, lines.length); j++) { const c = lines[j]; if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z'.\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'.\-]+){0,2}$/.test(c) && c.length <= 40 && !/zillow|clever|inquiry|property|follow|connection|premier|agent|message/i.test(c)) { name = c; break; } } } } } name = name.trim(); if (name) { const parts = name.split(/\s+/); out.firstName = parts.shift() || ''; out.lastName = parts.join(' '); } // SOURCE — detect the platform. if (/zillow/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Zillow'; else if (/clever/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Clever'; else if (/realtor\.com/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Realtor.com'; else if (/homelight/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'HomeLight'; else if (/redfin/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Redfin'; // NOTES — keep the readable text so the agent sees the full inquiry. out.notes = lines.slice(0, 70).join('\n').slice(0, 1800); return out; } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ZAPIER API LAYER (/api/v1/*) // Native Zapier integration endpoints. Auth = an agent's lead_token passed as // `Authorization: Bearer {token}` (same tokens used by /lead/{token}). This lets // Zapier: poll for new contacts (trigger), create contacts (action), and poll // for smart-plan step events (trigger) — replacing per-step pasted webhooks. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _apiCors() { return { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': ALLOWED_ORIGIN, 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization, X-Api-Key' }; } // Pull the bearer token (or X-Api-Key) off the request. function _apiToken(request) { const auth = request.headers.get('Authorization') || ''; if (/^Bearer\s+/i.test(auth)) return auth.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '').trim(); const k = request.headers.get('X-Api-Key'); if (k) return k.trim(); // also allow ?api_key= for easy browser testing try { const u = new URL(request.url); const q = u.searchParams.get('api_key'); if (q) return q.trim(); } catch (e) {} return ''; } // Resolve an API token → { agentId, teamId }. Returns null if invalid. async function _apiResolveToken(fbToken, apiKey) { if (!apiKey) return null; try { const tok = await fsGetDoc(fbToken, 'lead_tokens/' + apiKey); if (tok && tok.agentId) { let teamId = tok.teamId || ''; try { const sub = await fsGetDoc(fbToken, 'push_subscriptions/agent_' + tok.agentId); if (sub && sub.teamId) teamId = sub.teamId; } catch (e) {} return { agentId: tok.agentId, teamId: teamId }; } } catch (e) {} return null; } // GET /api/v1/auth-test → verifies the key, returns who it belongs to. async function apiAuthTest(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, agentId: who.agentId, teamId: who.teamId, label: 'NestTrack (' + who.agentId + ')' }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/contacts?since={ISO or ms}&limit= → recent contacts for this agent. // Zapier polls this for the "New Contact" trigger (newest first; Zapier dedupes by id). async function apiListContacts(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); let limit = 25; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const l = parseInt(u.searchParams.get('limit'), 10); if (l > 0 && l <= 100) limit = l; } catch (e) {} // Query users where agentId == who.agentId AND isContact == true. No orderBy // (avoids composite index) — we sort by createdAt in JS and cap. let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'isContact' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: true } } } ] } }, limit: 300 }); } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'query failed', detail: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return new Response(JSON.stringify([]), { status: 200, headers: cors }); const out = []; for (const r of rows) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); const id = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); out.push({ id: id, firstName: d.firstName || '', lastName: d.lastName || '', name: ((d.firstName || '') + ' ' + (d.lastName || '')).trim(), email: d.email || '', phone: d.phone || '', address: d.mailingAddress || '', source: d.source || '', segment: d.segment || '', status: d.crmStatus || '', type: d.journeyType || d.role || '', notes: d.notes || '', createdAt: d.createdAt || '', createdAtMs: d.createdAt ? Date.parse(d.createdAt) || 0 : 0 }); } out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs; }); // Optional ?since filter (ISO date or ms timestamp) let sinceMs = 0; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const s = u.searchParams.get('since'); if (s) sinceMs = /^\d+$/.test(s) ? parseInt(s, 10) : (Date.parse(s) || 0); } catch (e) {} const filtered = sinceMs ? out.filter(function (c) { return c.createdAtMs > sinceMs; }) : out; return new Response(JSON.stringify(filtered.slice(0, limit)), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/smartplan-events?since={ms}&limit= → recent smart-plan step events // for this agent. Zapier polls this for the "Smart Plan Step Reached" trigger. async function apiListSmartPlanEvents(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); let limit = 25; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const l = parseInt(u.searchParams.get('limit'), 10); if (l > 0 && l <= 100) limit = l; } catch (e) {} let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'zapier_events', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } }, limit: 200 }); } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'query failed', detail: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return new Response(JSON.stringify([]), { status: 200, headers: cors }); const out = []; for (const r of rows) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); const id = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); out.push({ id: id, planName: d.planName || '', stepLabel: d.stepLabel || '', triggerId: d.triggerId || '', firstName: d.firstName || '', lastName: d.lastName || '', name: ((d.firstName || '') + ' ' + (d.lastName || '')).trim(), email: d.email || '', phone: d.phone || '', address: d.mailingAddress || '', contactId: d.contactId || '', createdAtMs: typeof d.createdAtMs === 'number' ? d.createdAtMs : (d.createdAt ? Date.parse(d.createdAt) || 0 : 0) }); } out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs; }); let sinceMs = 0; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const s = u.searchParams.get('since'); if (s) sinceMs = /^\d+$/.test(s) ? parseInt(s, 10) : (Date.parse(s) || 0); } catch (e) {} let wantTrig = ''; try { const u = new URL(request.url); wantTrig = (u.searchParams.get('triggerId') || '').trim(); } catch (e) {} let filtered = sinceMs ? out.filter(function (e) { return e.createdAtMs > sinceMs; }) : out; if (wantTrig) filtered = filtered.filter(function (e) { return e.triggerId === wantTrig; }); return new Response(JSON.stringify(filtered.slice(0, limit)), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/deals?since={ms}&limit= → recent deals for this agent. // POST /api/v1/homebot-enroll -> the app calls this when an agent taps "Add to Homebot". // Writes a homebot_enrollments doc scoped to THIS agent. Zapier polls it (dedicated trigger). // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // NESTTRACKER — ATTOM AVM home valuation (Phase 1) // Core: given a split address, fetch ATTOM's AVM (value, range, confidence). // Endpoint: /propertyapi/v1.0.0/avm/detail, header `apikey`, params address1/address2. // Confidence: ATTOM returns avm.amount.scr (0–100). Gate below NESTTRACKER_MIN_CONFIDENCE. // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── const NESTTRACKER_MIN_CONFIDENCE = 88; // tuned from Cleveland testing (good results 91–95; bad one 82) async function attomGetValue(env, parts) { const key = env.ATTOM_API_KEY; if (!key) return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM_API_KEY not configured on the server' }; const street = (parts && parts.street || '').trim(); const cityStateZip = [ (parts && parts.city || '').trim(), [ (parts && parts.state || '').trim(), (parts && parts.zip || '').trim() ].filter(Boolean).join(' ') ].filter(Boolean).join(', '); if (!street || !cityStateZip) return { ok:false, reason:'need a street and city/state/zip to value a home' }; const url = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/propertyapi/v1.0.0/avm/detail' + '?address1=' + encodeURIComponent(street) + '&address2=' + encodeURIComponent(cityStateZip); try { const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'apikey': key, 'accept': 'application/json' } }); if (!r.ok) { const t = await r.text(); // ATTOM 400 "SuccessWithoutResult" = well-formed request, no property matched that address. if (r.status === 400 && /SuccessWithoutResult|"total":0/i.test(t)){ return { ok:false, reason:"We couldn't find that exact address in the property database. Double-check the street number, spelling, and ZIP — or pick it from the address suggestions so it matches.", queried:{ address1: street, address2: cityStateZip } }; } return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM ' + r.status + ': ' + String(t).slice(0,120) }; } const d = await r.json(); const prop = (d.property && d.property[0]) || null; const amt = prop && prop.avm && prop.avm.amount; if (!amt || amt.value == null) { return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM has no valuation for this address', matched: !!prop }; } const confidence = (amt.scr != null) ? Number(amt.scr) : null; const calc = (prop.avm && prop.avm.calculations) || {}; const addr = (prop.address) || {}; return { ok: true, value: Number(amt.value), high: amt.high != null ? Number(amt.high) : null, low: amt.low != null ? Number(amt.low) : null, confidence: confidence, lowConfidence: (confidence != null && confidence < NESTTRACKER_MIN_CONFIDENCE), monthlyChangePct: (calc.monthlyChgPct != null) ? Number(calc.monthlyChgPct) : null, attomId: (prop.identifier && prop.identifier.attomId) || null, matchedAddress: addr.oneLine || null, raw: { value: amt.value, high: amt.high, low: amt.low, scr: amt.scr } }; } catch (e) { return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM request error: ' + (e && e.message || 'network') }; } } // ── Provider dispatcher: swapping AVM providers later is a config flip (env.AVM_PROVIDER) ── async function getHomeValue(env, parts) { const provider = String(env.AVM_PROVIDER || 'attom').toLowerCase(); // Future providers plug in here, each returning the same normalized shape: // if (provider === 'housecanary') return await housecanaryGetValue(env, parts); return await attomGetValue(env, parts); } // ── Free equity estimate: no API cost. Amortizes an assumed loan from purchase price + date. ── // Assumes 20% down, 30-yr fixed, rate by purchase year. Rough by design — labeled "estimated". function _rateForYear(y){ var t = { 2000:8.05,2001:6.97,2002:6.54,2003:5.83,2004:5.84,2005:5.87,2006:6.41,2007:6.34,2008:6.03,2009:5.04,2010:4.69,2011:4.45,2012:3.66,2013:3.98,2014:4.17,2015:3.85,2016:3.65,2017:3.99,2018:4.54,2019:3.94,2020:3.11,2021:2.96,2022:5.34,2023:6.81,2024:6.72,2025:6.80,2026:6.70 }; if (y && t[y]!=null) return t[y]/100; return 0.06; // fallback } // Remaining balance on an amortizing loan after `monthsPaid` of a `termMonths` loan at annual `rate`. // Standard amortization: B = P * [(1+i)^n - (1+i)^p] / [(1+i)^n - 1]. rate as a percent (e.g. 6.5). function _amortBalance(loanAmount, annualRatePct, termMonths, monthsPaid){ loanAmount = Number(loanAmount)||0; termMonths = Number(termMonths)||360; monthsPaid = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(monthsPaid)||0, termMonths)); if (!loanAmount) return 0; var i = (Number(annualRatePct)||0)/100/12; var bal; if (i > 0){ bal = loanAmount * (Math.pow(1+i, termMonths) - Math.pow(1+i, monthsPaid)) / (Math.pow(1+i, termMonths) - 1); } else { bal = loanAmount * (1 - monthsPaid/termMonths); // 0% → straight-line } return Math.max(0, Math.round(bal)); } // Months between a start date (ISO/date string or year) and now. function _monthsSince(startStr){ if (!startStr) return 0; var d = null; if (/^\d{4}$/.test(String(startStr).trim())) d = new Date(Number(startStr), 0, 1); else { d = new Date(startStr); } if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return 0; return Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.now() - d.getTime()) / (30.44*86400000))); } function estimateEquity(currentValue, purchasePrice, purchaseDate, loan){ currentValue = Number(currentValue)||0; loan = loan || {}; // Real loan data path (agent/lender entered loan amount + rate + term): amortize precisely. var loanAmount = Number(loan.loanAmount)||0; if (currentValue && loanAmount){ var term = (String(loan.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; var start = loan.loanStart || loan.purchaseDate || purchaseDate || ''; var monthsPaid = _monthsSince(start); var ratePct; if (loan.ownerRate!=null && String(loan.ownerRate)!==''){ ratePct = Number(loan.ownerRate); // agent entered a percent, e.g. 6.5 } else { var yr = null; if (start){ var sd = /^\d{4}$/.test(String(start).trim()) ? new Date(Number(start),0,1) : new Date(start); if(sd && !isNaN(sd.getTime())) yr = sd.getFullYear(); } ratePct = (_rateForYear(yr)||0.06) * 100; // _rateForYear returns a decimal → percent } var bal = _amortBalance(loanAmount, ratePct, term, monthsPaid); var eq = Math.max(0, Math.round(currentValue - bal)); return { estimated: false, fromLoanData: true, estLoanBalance: bal, equity: eq, equityPct: currentValue ? Math.round((eq/currentValue)*100) : null, loanAmount: loanAmount, loanTerm: (term===180?'15':'30'), ownerRate: (ratePct!=null?Math.round(ratePct*1000)/1000:null), loanStart: start || null }; } // Fallback estimate (no loan data): assume 20% down, 30-yr, rate-by-year — unchanged legacy behavior. purchasePrice = Number(purchasePrice)||0; if (!currentValue || !purchasePrice) return null; var pd = purchaseDate ? new Date(purchaseDate) : null; var monthsElapsed = 0, year = null; if (pd && !isNaN(pd.getTime())) { year = pd.getFullYear(); monthsElapsed = Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.now() - pd.getTime()) / (30.44*86400000))); } var loanF = purchasePrice * 0.80; var balance = _amortBalance(loanF, (_rateForYear(year)||0)*100, 360, monthsElapsed); var equity = Math.max(0, Math.round(currentValue - balance)); return { estimated: true, estLoanBalance: balance, equity: equity, equityPct: currentValue ? Math.round((equity/currentValue)*100) : null, assumedDownPct: 20, basedOn: { purchasePrice: purchasePrice, purchaseYear: year } }; } // ── Equity insight ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Turns the raw equity numbers into the two things a homeowner actually cares about: // 1. growth — how much of this year's gain came from the market vs. paying the loan down // 2. hook — the single most useful thing worth a conversation about these numbers // Pure and synchronous. The emailed quarterly report and the in-app home dashboard both // render from this, so they can never disagree with each other. function _monthlyPayment(principal, annualRatePct, termMonths){ principal = Number(principal)||0; termMonths = Number(termMonths)||0; var i = (Number(annualRatePct)||0)/100/12; if (!principal || termMonths <= 0) return 0; if (i === 0) return principal/termMonths; var f = Math.pow(1+i, termMonths); return principal * i * f / (f - 1); } // Newest stored value at or before N months ago. Returns null when no valuation that old exists, // so the caller can relabel rather than quietly measure from the purchase price and call it a year. function _valueMonthsAgo(history, months){ if (!Array.isArray(history) || !history.length) return null; var cutoff = Date.now() - months*30.44*86400000; var best = null, bestT = 0; for (var i=0;i cutoff) continue; if (t > bestT){ best = h; bestT = t; } } return best ? (Number(best.value)||null) : null; } var PMI_ANNUAL_PCT = 0.005; // ~0.5%/yr of the original loan — typical; always label it "around" var PMI_EQUITY_PCT = 20; // where asking the lender to drop PMI becomes worth doing var PMI_CLOSE_PCT = 15; // close enough that it's worth telling them it's coming var REFI_MIN_DELTA = 0.75; // percentage points of rate improvement before it's worth a call function equityInsight(eq, home, history, currentValue, rate30){ if (!eq || !currentValue) return null; home = home || {}; // These are stored as free text ('$375,000'). Number() on that is NaN, which silently nulls // downPct and makes the PMI hook unreachable. Strip to digits, as the report endpoint does. var _n = function(v){ return Number(String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) || 0; }; var purchasePrice = _n(home.purchasePrice); var loanAmount = _n(home.loanAmount); var termMonths = (String(eq.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; var monthsOwned = _monthsSince(eq.loanStart || home.purchaseDate || ''); // Growth split. We only say "this year" when there's a real valuation from a year ago to // measure against. Otherwise the honest frame is "since you bought". var basis = 'purchase', windowMonths = Math.max(0, monthsOwned), thenValue = purchasePrice; if (monthsOwned >= 12){ var yearAgo = _valueMonthsAgo(history, 12); if (yearAgo){ basis = 'year'; windowMonths = 12; thenValue = yearAgo; } } var appreciation = (thenValue && currentValue) ? Math.round(currentValue - thenValue) : 0; var principalPaid = 0; if (eq.fromLoanData && loanAmount && monthsOwned > 0){ var balThen = _amortBalance(loanAmount, eq.ownerRate, termMonths, Math.max(0, monthsOwned - windowMonths)); principalPaid = Math.max(0, Math.round(balThen - eq.estLoanBalance)); } var growth = { basis:basis, months:windowMonths, appreciation:appreciation, principalPaid:principalPaid, total:appreciation + principalPaid }; // The hook. First one that fires wins — they're ordered by how much money is on the table. // PMI and refi are gated on real loan data; we never make a financial claim off an assumption. var equityPct = Number(eq.equityPct)||0; var downPct = (purchasePrice && loanAmount) ? Math.round(((purchasePrice - loanAmount)/purchasePrice)*100) : null; var real = !!eq.fromLoanData; var hook; if (real && downPct!=null && downPct < 20 && equityPct >= PMI_EQUITY_PCT){ hook = { type:'pmi_ready', monthly: Math.round((loanAmount*PMI_ANNUAL_PCT)/12), downPct:downPct, equityPct:equityPct }; } else if (real && downPct!=null && downPct < 20 && equityPct >= PMI_CLOSE_PCT){ hook = { type:'pmi_close', away: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentValue*(PMI_EQUITY_PCT/100) - eq.equity)), downPct:downPct, equityPct:equityPct }; } else if (real && rate30 && eq.ownerRate && (eq.ownerRate - rate30) >= REFI_MIN_DELTA){ var remaining = Math.max(1, termMonths - monthsOwned); var save = _monthlyPayment(eq.estLoanBalance, eq.ownerRate, remaining) - _monthlyPayment(eq.estLoanBalance, rate30, remaining); hook = { type:'refi', monthly: Math.max(0, Math.round(save)), yourRate: eq.ownerRate, todayRate: rate30 }; } else if (equityPct >= 35){ hook = { type:'equity_strong', equityPct:equityPct, equity:eq.equity }; } else { hook = { type:'baseline', equityPct:equityPct }; } return { growth:growth, hook:hook, monthsOwned:monthsOwned, downPct:downPct, estimated: !real }; } // ── Home maintenance ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Six defaults, not a checklist. Every one is a starting point the homeowner can re-time or // switch off in a single tap, and off means off. Completing a task writes a log entry and // reschedules from the day it was actually done — never from the day the calendar guessed. // The log is the product: eleven taps a year becomes a maintenance record nobody set out to keep. var NT_TASKS = [ { key:'furnace_filter', title:'Change your furnace filter', everyDays:90, seedDays:30, askSpec:true }, { key:'hvac_service', title:'Have your HVAC serviced', everyDays:182, seasonal:true, pro:'HVAC' }, { key:'water_filter', title:'Change your water filter', everyDays:182, seedDays:90, askSpec:true }, { key:'smoke_co', title:'Change your smoke and CO detector batteries', everyDays:182, seedDays:180 }, { key:'gutters', title:'Have your gutters cleaned', everyDays:182, seasonal:true, pro:'Gutters' }, { key:'water_heater', title:'Flush your water heater', everyDays:1095, seedDays:180, pro:'Plumbing' }, // Anniversary-anchored: timed off the closing date, not the calendar. Skipped entirely // when we only have a purchase YEAR, because the renewal day would be a guess. { key:'insurance', title:'Home insurance renewal', everyDays:365, anniversary:true, offsetDays:-30, pro:'Insurance' }, { key:'home_anniversary', title:'Home anniversary', everyDays:365, anniversary:true, offsetDays:0 } ]; function _ymd(ms){ return new Date(ms).toISOString().slice(0,10); } // Seasonal work lands in spring or fall, whichever comes first, never sooner than a month out. // (Spring/fall are national approximations. A climate-zone table would sharpen this later.) function _nextSeasonal(fromMs){ var floor = fromMs + 30*86400000; var y = new Date(floor).getUTCFullYear(); var cands = [Date.UTC(y,3,15), Date.UTC(y,9,15), Date.UTC(y+1,3,15)]; for (var i=0;i= floor) return _ymd(cands[i]); } return _ymd(cands[2]); } // The schedule starts when the schedule starts. Seeding from the purchase date looked right for a // fresh close, but a past client enrolled years later would wake up four years overdue on every // task and get pushed about all of them at once. // Given an anchor month (0-11) and a frequency in days, find the next occurrence of that month at // least 30 days out. For sub-yearly frequencies (6mo) the anchor month plus the interval naturally // gives the pair — Oct + 6mo lands Apr, then Oct again. // Next occurrence of the closing month/day, at least a week out, shifted by offsetDays // (-30 for the insurance heads-up, 0 for the anniversary itself). Returns null when the // stored date is a bare year or unparseable — the caller then skips the task entirely. function _nextAnniversary(dateStr, offsetDays){ var s = String(dateStr||'').trim(); if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return null; var d = new Date(s.slice(0,10) + 'T12:00:00Z'); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return null; var mo = d.getUTCMonth(), day = d.getUTCDate(); var floor = Date.now() + 7*86400000; var y0 = new Date(floor).getUTCFullYear(); for (var i=0; i<3; i++){ var c = Date.UTC(y0+i, mo, day) + (Number(offsetDays)||0)*86400000; if (c >= floor) return _ymd(c); } return null; } // Whole years since closing, for {{YEARS}} in the anniversary copy. function _yearsOwned(dateStr){ var s = String(dateStr||'').trim(); var d = /^\d{4}$/.test(s) ? new Date(Number(s),0,1) : new Date(s.slice(0,10)+'T12:00:00Z'); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return 0; return Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - d.getTime())/(365.25*86400000))); } function _yearsWord(n){ var w = ['zero','one','two','three','four','five','six','seven','eight','nine','ten']; return (n <= 10 ? w[n] : String(n)) + ' year' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's'); } function _nextAnchoredMonth(fromMs, month, everyDays){ var floor = fromMs + 30*86400000; // yearly-or-longer: land on the anchor month; sub-yearly: nearest upcoming anchor, interval handles the rest var y = new Date(floor).getUTCFullYear(); var cands = [Date.UTC(y, month, 15), Date.UTC(y+1, month, 15), Date.UTC(y+2, month, 15)]; for (var i=0;i= floor) return _ymd(cands[i]); } return _ymd(cands[0]); } // settings: optional per-agent map { hvac_service:{months,freqDays,off}, gutters:{...}, water_heater:{...} }. // Absent or malformed → behave exactly as before. This runs at enrollment; a bad settings read must // never produce an empty schedule, so every branch falls back to the global task default. function ntSeedTasks(settings, purchaseDate){ settings = settings || {}; var base = Date.now(); var out = []; NT_TASKS.forEach(function(t){ var s = settings[t.key] || null; // Anniversary tasks: one path, no month or seasonal logic. No usable closing date, // no task — a guessed renewal date in a client's inbox is worse than no email. if (t.anniversary){ if (s && s.off === true) return; var due = _nextAnniversary(purchaseDate, t.offsetDays); if (!due) return; out.push({ key:t.key, title:t.title, everyDays:365, enabled:true, spec:'', pro:t.pro||'', askSpec:false, anniversary:true, offsetDays:(t.offsetDays||0), nextDue:due }); return; } // Only the three big-ticket tasks are agent-configurable; the DIY ones ignore settings entirely. var configurable = !!MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS[t.key]; if (configurable && s){ if (s.off === true) return; // agent turned this reminder off var every = (Number(s.freqDays) > 0) ? Number(s.freqDays) : t.everyDays; var month = (s.month != null && s.month >= 0 && s.month <= 11) ? Number(s.month) : null; out.push({ key:t.key, title:t.title, everyDays:every, enabled:true, spec:'', pro:t.pro||'', askSpec:!!t.askSpec, nextDue: (month != null) ? _nextAnchoredMonth(base, month, every) : (t.seasonal ? _nextSeasonal(base) : _ymd(base + (t.seedDays||30)*86400000)) }); return; } // Default (untouched agent, or non-configurable DIY task) — exactly as before. out.push({ key:t.key, title:t.title, everyDays:t.everyDays, enabled:true, spec:'', pro:t.pro||'', askSpec:!!t.askSpec, nextDue: t.seasonal ? _nextSeasonal(base) : _ymd(base + (t.seedDays||30)*86400000) }); }); return out; } var NT_TASK_BY_KEY = {}; NT_TASKS.forEach(function(t){ NT_TASK_BY_KEY[t.key] = t; }); // Load an agent's per-task maintenance settings (month + frequency + off) from their config doc. // Any failure returns {} → seeding falls back to global defaults. Never throws into the seed path. async function _ntMaintSettings(token, agentId){ if (!agentId) return {}; try { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agents/'+agentId+'/config/maintSchedule'); if (!doc) return {}; var out = {}; ['hvac_service','gutters','water_heater','insurance','home_anniversary'].forEach(function(k){ var v = doc[k]; if (!v) return; out[k] = { off: v.off === true, month: (v.month != null ? Number(v.month) : null), freqDays: (v.freqDays != null ? Number(v.freqDays) : null) }; }); return out; } catch(e){ return {}; } } // The per-home token is the auth, exactly as it is for the report. async function _ntLoadHome(env, tok){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok); if (!home || home.active === false) return { token:token, home:null }; var tasks=[], log=[], items=[]; try { tasks = home.tasksJson ? JSON.parse(home.tasksJson) : []; } catch(e){} try { log = home.logJson ? JSON.parse(home.logJson) : []; } catch(e){} try { items = home.itemsJson ? JSON.parse(home.itemsJson) : []; } catch(e){} return { token:token, home:home, tasks:tasks, log:log, items:items }; } // GET /api/v1/home-tasks?t=TOKEN → PUBLIC. Returns everything the home dashboard needs in one call. async function apiHomeTasks(request, env, cors){ let tok=''; try { tok = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('t')||'').trim(); } catch(e){} if (!tok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing token'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const s = await _ntLoadHome(env, tok); if (!s.home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); const on = (s.home.homeDash !== false); // The homeowner is looking at their report right now. Recorded but not awaited — tracking // must never delay or break the thing it is measuring. // Send history, so the agent can see when the last report went and when the next is due. // Both derive from lastPulledAt, which the quarterly sweep already maintains — nothing new // to store, it simply was not being handed back. let _lastSent = null, _nextDue = null; try { const lp = s.home.lastPulledAt; const ms = lp && lp.seconds ? lp.seconds * 1000 : (lp ? Date.parse(lp) : 0); if (ms) { _lastSent = new Date(ms).toISOString(); _nextDue = new Date(ms + 89*24*60*60*1000).toISOString(); } } catch(e) {} return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, homeDash:on, tasks:(on ? s.tasks : []), log:s.log, items:s.items, lastSentAt:_lastSent, nextDueAt:_nextDue, views:(Number(s.home.views)||0) }),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // Deliberately arithmetic, not AI. Every input is a fact the homeowner produced, and the // reasons are shown alongside the score \u2014 an agent should be able to see why someone surfaced // and disagree with it. A black-box number nobody trusts gets ignored within a week. function _ntScore(home) { let score = 0; const why = []; let recent = []; try { recent = JSON.parse(home.recentViewsJson || '[]'); } catch (e) {} const totalViews = Number(home.viewCount) || 0; const today = Math.floor(Date.now() / 86400000); const last7 = recent.filter(function (d) { return d > today - 7; }).length; const last30 = recent.length; // Repeat visits in a short window are the strongest passive signal. if (last7 >= 3) { score += 40; why.push('opened ' + last7 + ' times this week'); } else if (last7 === 2) { score += 25; why.push('opened twice this week'); } else if (last7 === 1) { score += 10; why.push('opened this week'); } else if (last30 >= 3) { score += 15; why.push('opened ' + last30 + ' times this month'); } else if (last30 >= 1) { score += 5; why.push('opened ' + (last30 > 1 ? last30 + ' times ' : '') + 'this month'); } // They told you something. Worth more than any inference. if (home.lastIntent) { const i = String(home.lastIntent).toLowerCase(); if (/ready|now|net sheet/.test(i)) { score += 45; why.push(home.lastIntent); } else if (/soon|1.?2|6.?12/.test(i)) { score += 30; why.push(home.lastIntent); } else { score += 10; why.push(home.lastIntent); } } // Equity is what makes a move possible. A large jump is a reason to call today. const eq = Number(home.estEquity) || 0; const eqPrev = Number(home.estEquityPrev) || 0; if (eq >= 150000) { score += 15; why.push('$' + Math.round(eq / 1000) + 'k equity'); } if (eqPrev && eq > eqPrev * 1.1) { score += 20; why.push('equity up $' + Math.round((eq - eqPrev) / 1000) + 'k since last report'); } // Seven years is roughly when people start looking, and the median seller has now been in // place eleven. Weak on its own; useful stacked with anything else. const yrs = Number(home.yearsOwned) || 0; if (yrs >= 7) { score += 10; why.push(yrs + ' years in the home'); } // Never opened it. Not a seller signal \u2014 a re-engagement one, and worth separating. if (!recent.length && totalViews === 0) { return { score: 0, why: ['never opened a report'], cold: true }; } return { score: Math.min(score, 100), why: why, cold: false }; } // GET /api/v1/nt-signals \u2014 an agent's enrolled homeowners, ranked by how likely they look to // be thinking about moving. This is the piece Fello charges for: the reports themselves have // existed here for months, what was missing is the ranked "who to call" list on top. // // Scoring runs server-side so the app fetches a short ordered list rather than every home and // its history. An agent with 400 enrolled homeowners should get eight names, not 400 records. function _srNotFoundHtml(msg) { return '' + '
' + '
\uD83D\uDCC4
' + '

This report is no longer available

' + '

' + (msg || 'Reports are kept while the home is on the market and removed once the sale closes. Ask your agent for the latest one.') + '

' + '
'; } // Banner above the stored report. It is immutable by design \u2014 the numbers were true on the day // it was sent, and quietly refreshing them would undermine the point of a weekly report. function _srDatedHtml(inner, sentDateStr, address) { let when = ''; try { if (sentDateStr) { const d = new Date(sentDateStr + 'T12:00:00'); if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) { when = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } } } catch (e) {} const banner = '
' + '
' + (when ? 'This update was sent ' + when + '' : 'This is a saved update') + (address ? ' for ' + String(address).replace(/[<>]/g, '') : '') + '.
It shows the numbers as they were that day, not today.' + '
'; return '' + '' + '' + 'Listing Update' + '' + banner + inner + ''; } // \u2500\u2500 MCP server \u2500\u2500 // Lets an assistant read the working state of an agent's business \u2014 to-dos, deals, the day's // appointments, follow-ups \u2014 alongside their inbox and calendar, so a morning briefing can say // "you have three deals closing this week and nobody has called the Kowalskis in nine days" // rather than just reading out email. // // Streamable HTTP transport (JSON-RPC over a single POST endpoint), which is what Claude's // custom connectors expect. Anthropic's cloud makes the connection, not the local machine, so // the endpoint is public and the token in the path is what protects it. // // Reads are broad; the only write is adding a to-do. Moving a deal or emailing a client stays // a human decision \u2014 an assistant that can reorganise a pipeline is a much bigger trust // question than one that can leave a note. const MCP_TOOLS = [ { name: 'get_todos', description: "The agent's open to-do list, newest first. Includes anything scheduled for a date, and which client each item relates to.", inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { include_done: { type: 'boolean', description: 'Include completed items. Defaults to false.' } } } }, { name: 'add_todo', description: "Add a to-do for the agent. Use for follow-ups the agent asked to be reminded about, or a task that came out of an email. Does not notify anyone.", inputSchema: { type: 'object', required: ['text'], properties: { text: { type: 'string', description: 'What needs doing, in the agent\u2019s own terms.' }, date: { type: 'string', description: 'Optional due date, YYYY-MM-DD.' }, time: { type: 'string', description: 'Optional time, HH:MM 24-hour.' } } } }, { name: 'get_deals', description: 'Live transactions with their stage, address, client and closing date. This is the pipeline \u2014 use it to say what is closing soon or what has been sitting.', inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { status: { type: 'string', description: "Filter by status, e.g. 'Under Contract'. Omit for all open deals." } } } }, { name: 'get_appointments', description: "Showings and appointments in a date window. Use for the day's schedule.", inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { days: { type: 'number', description: 'How many days ahead. Defaults to 1 (today).' } } } }, { name: 'get_clients', description: 'Clients with their status, assigned agent and lender, and where they are in their journey. Use to answer questions about a specific person.', inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { status: { type: 'string', description: "Filter by CRM status, e.g. 'active', 'pipeline', 'nurture'." }, search: { type: 'string', description: 'Match on name or email.' } } } }, { name: 'get_signals', description: 'What clients have actually done lately \u2014 listings they loved or passed on, showing requests, homeowners repeatedly opening their value report. This is the part no other CRM can tell you, and it is usually the most useful thing in a briefing.', inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { days: { type: 'number', description: 'How far back to look. Defaults to 7.' } } } } ]; // Firestore needs every value typed. The app writes this array through the client SDK, which // does the encoding for you; from the worker it has to be explicit or the whole list is lost. function _mcpTodoArray(items) { return { arrayValue: { values: (items || []).map(function (t) { return { mapValue: { fields: { id: { stringValue: String(t.id || '') }, text: { stringValue: String(t.text || '') }, date: { stringValue: String(t.date || '') }, time: { stringValue: String(t.time || '') }, done: { booleanValue: !!t.done }, createdAt: { integerValue: String(t.createdAt || Date.now()) }, clientUid: t.clientUid ? { stringValue: String(t.clientUid) } : { nullValue: null }, clientName: t.clientName ? { stringValue: String(t.clientName) } : { nullValue: null }, source: { stringValue: String(t.source || 'agent') } } } }; }) } }; } function _mcpResult(id, result) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: id, result: result }), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); } function _mcpError(id, code, message) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: id, error: { code: code, message: message } }), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); } // Tool results are text blocks. JSON keeps them unambiguous \u2014 prose would invite the model to // re-derive numbers it was handed. function _mcpText(obj) { return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) }] }; } async function handleMcp(request, env, pathToken) { if (request.method === 'GET') { // Some clients probe with GET before opening a session. return new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 'ok', transport: 'streamable-http' }), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); } if (request.method !== 'POST') return new Response('Method not allowed', { status: 405 }); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { return _mcpError(null, -32700, 'Parse error'); } const id = (body && body.id !== undefined) ? body.id : null; const method = body && body.method; if (method === 'initialize') { return _mcpResult(id, { protocolVersion: '2025-06-18', capabilities: { tools: {} }, serverInfo: { name: 'nesttrack', version: '1.0.0' } }); } // Notifications carry no id and expect no result. if (method === 'notifications/initialized') return new Response('', { status: 202 }); if (method === 'tools/list') return _mcpResult(id, { tools: MCP_TOOLS }); if (method !== 'tools/call') return _mcpError(id, -32601, 'Method not found: ' + method); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, pathToken); if (!who || !who.agentId) return _mcpError(id, -32001, 'Unknown or expired NestTrack token.'); const name = body.params && body.params.name; const args = (body.params && body.params.arguments) || {}; try { const out = await _mcpCall(name, args, token, env, who); return _mcpResult(id, _mcpText(out)); } catch (e) { console.log('mcp ' + name + ':', e && e.message); return _mcpResult(id, { content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'That failed: ' + (e && e.message) }], isError: true }); } } async function _mcpCall(name, args, token, env, who) { const agentId = who.agentId; if (name === 'get_todos') { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_todos/' + who.uid).catch(function(){ return null; }) }; } if (name === 'add_todo') { if (!args.text || !String(args.text).trim()) throw new Error('text is required'); const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_todos/' + who.uid).catch(function(){ return null; Called when block payment status changes (purchase, lapse, restore). Sets a // `locked` bool on each home — the sweep skips locked homes, and reports show a paused state. // // FAIL-OPEN by design (the lesson from the platform billing gate): if we can't read the agent's // payment state, we DO NOT lock anything. A lock only ever happens on a definitive, successfully-read // over-cap condition — never because a lookup hiccuped. async function _ntReconcileAgentLocks(token, agentId, env){ // 1) Resolve the agent's payment state ONCE.
