rest = rest.slice(0, sm.index).trim().replace(/,\s*$/, ''); } } if (rest.indexOf(',') !== -1) { const p = rest.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean); if (p.length >= 2) { city = aclean(p[p.length-1]); street = aclean(p.slice(0, p.length-1).join(', ')); } else if (p.length === 1) { street = aclean(p[0]); } } else { const m = rest.match(STREET_TYPES); if (m) { let idx = m.index + m[0].length; if (rest[idx]==='.') idx++; street = aclean(rest.slice(0, idx)); city = aclean(rest.slice(idx)); } else { street = aclean(rest); } } } if (!street || !zip) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'Need a full property address (street + ZIP) to value this home.' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); // Re-pull of an already-enrolled home passes ntToken and skips the cap check. const existingToken = String(c.ntToken || '').trim(); // Two DIFFERENT people, two DIFFERENT jobs: // • brandAgentId — whose name is on the report/email. The contact's ASSIGNED agent from the // client card. Defaults to the enroller when the contact has no explicit agent. // • who.agentId — the ENROLLER (from the API key). This is whose CAP the home counts against, // no matter who's assigned. A lender enrolling 40 homes for various agents burns the LENDER's // cap, never the agents'. Cap ownership follows the click, branding follows the assignment. const brandAgentId = String(c.agentId || '').trim() || who.agentId; if (!existingToken) { // Anti-spam gate: NestTracker emails are marketing mail and legally need the agent's physical // mailing address in the footer. Block new enrollment until they've set one in their profile. // A P.O. Box or brokerage office address works — surfaced in the message so it's not a dead end. try { const _ag = await _ntResolveAgent(token, brandAgentId); if (!((_ag && _ag.mailingAddress) || '').trim()) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'Add your business mailing address in Profile before enrolling homes — anti-spam law requires it on the emails NestTracker sends for you. A P.O. Box or your brokerage office address works; you don\'t have to use your home address.', needAddress:true }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } } catch(e){} const activeCount = await _ntActiveCount(token, who.agentId); const effCap = await _ntEffectiveCap(token, who.agentId); if (activeCount >= effCap) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'You\'ve reached your '+effCap+'-home limit. Upgrade for more, or remove some to add another.', capReached:true, cap:effCap }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } } const parts = { street, city, state, zip }; const purchasePrice = String(c.purchasePrice || c.salePrice || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); const purchaseDate = String(c.purchaseDate || c.closingDate || '').trim(); // Loan details (agent/lender entered). Any subset is fine; loanAmount is the trigger for amortized equity. const loanAmount = String(c.loanAmount || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); const ownerRate = String(c.ownerRate || c.interestRate || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); const loanTerm = (String(c.loanTerm||'').trim() === '15') ? '15' : (c.loanTerm ? '30' : ''); const loanStart = String(c.loanStart || c.loanStartDate || '').trim() || purchaseDate; const loanObj = { loanAmount: loanAmount, ownerRate: ownerRate, loanTerm: loanTerm || '30', loanStart: loanStart, purchaseDate: purchaseDate }; // A directly-entered current balance (ownerOwed) wins over amortized estimate for the starting snapshot. const ownerOwedIn = String(c.ownerOwed || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); let report = await getHomeReport(env, parts, { comps: true, purchasePrice: purchasePrice, purchaseDate: purchaseDate, loan: loanObj }); // If ATTOM has no automated value (common for duplexes / 2-4 unit / new construction / unusual // properties), don't hard-fail — let the agent/lender enter the value manually. We create a pending // home with value 0 and flag needsManualValue; the preview collects the number and confirm's existing // overrideValue path finalizes it (valueSource:'agent', drifts by ZIP trend thereafter). let needsManualValue = false; if (!report.ok){ needsManualValue = true; // any AVM miss → let the agent enter the value; confirm handles the rest } // If the agent gave an explicit current balance, override the computed equity with it at enroll. if (report.ok && ownerOwedIn !== '' && report.value){ const oo = Number(ownerOwedIn); if (!isNaN(oo)){ report.equity = report.equity || {}; report.equity.estLoanBalance = oo; report.equity.equity = Math.max(0, report.value - oo); report.equity.equityPct = report.value ? Math.round(report.equity.equity/report.value*100) : null; report.equity.estimated = false; } } // Normalize a failed report into a zero-value shell so the rest of the enroll write works unchanged. // (needsManualValue is already set above; the agent will supply the value in the preview.) if (!report.ok){ report = { ok:true, value:0, high:0, low:0, confidence:0, lowConfidence:true, monthlyChangePct:null, matchedAddress:'', equity:null, comps:[] }; } const homeToken = existingToken || ('nt_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)); const nowIso = new Date().toISOString(); const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); // History: one entry per quarter (keeps the trend). On re-pull, read + preserve prior entries. let history = []; if (existingToken) { try { const prev = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + existingToken); if (prev && prev.historyJson) history = JSON.parse(prev.historyJson) || []; } catch(e){} } history = history.filter(function(h){ return h && h.q !== q; }); history.push({ q: q, value: report.value, ts: nowIso }); if (history.length > 24) history = history.slice(history.length - 24); const isNew = !existingToken; const fields = { agentId: fsString(brandAgentId), enrolledBy: fsString(who.agentId), // whose CAP this counts against (the enroller) — separate from branding contactUid: fsString(contactId), lenderId: fsString(lenderId), // assigned lender for co-branding; mirrors the client card (blank = agent-only) homeDash: fsBool(!!(c.setupHome != null ? c.setupHome : body.setupHome)), // explicit, never inferred emailsOff: fsBool(false), // (re-)enrolling is (re-)subscribing — clears any prior unsubscribe so pulls resume firstName: fsString(firstName), lastName: fsString(lastName), email: fsString(email), mailingAddress: fsString(address), street: fsString(street), city: fsString(city), state: fsString(state), zip: fsString(zip), purchasePrice: fsString(purchasePrice), purchaseDate: fsString(purchaseDate), // Loan details for quarterly amortization (paydown). Blank → equity tracks value only. loanAmount: fsString(loanAmount), ownerRate: fsString(ownerRate), loanTerm: fsString(loanTerm || (loanAmount ? '30' : '')), loanStart: fsString(loanStart), ownerOwed: fsString(ownerOwedIn), // NEW enrollments start PENDING (active:false) until the agent confirms in the preview. // Pending homes don't count toward the cap and are never sent — confirm flips them live. active: fsBool(!isNew), pending: fsBool(isNew), value: fsInt(report.value || 0), refAvm: fsInt(report.value || 0), high: fsInt(report.high || 0), low: fsInt(report.low || 0), confidence: fsInt(report.confidence || 0), lowConfidence: fsBool(!!report.lowConfidence), monthlyChangePct: fsString(report.monthlyChangePct == null ? '' : String(report.monthlyChangePct)), matchedAddress: fsString(report.matchedAddress || ''), equityJson: fsString(report.equity ? JSON.stringify(report.equity) : ''), compsJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(report.comps || [])), compsPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), historyJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(history)), lastPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), lastPulledQuarter: fsString(q) }; if (!existingToken) fields.enrolledAt = fsTimestamp(); try { await firestoreWrite(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + homeToken, fields); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'Could not save enrollment: ' + e.message }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, preview: isNew, // NEW enroll → preview (needs confirm); legacy re-pull → live needsManualValue: needsManualValue, // true when ATTOM had no AVM — preview must collect a value token: homeToken, value: report.value, high: report.high, low: report.low, confidence: report.confidence, lowConfidence: !!report.lowConfidence, matchedAddress: report.matchedAddress || '', equity: report.equity ? report.equity.equity : null, equityPct: report.equity ? report.equity.equityPct : null, compsCount: (report.comps || []).length, reportUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/report.html?t=' + homeToken }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-confirm → commit a pending preview live (agent decided). Optional // overrideValue lets the agent correct a bad AVM. NO new ATTOM pull — reuses the preview's data. async function apiNestTrackerConfirm(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch(e){} const tokenId = String(body.token || body.t || '').trim(); if (!tokenId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'missing token' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + tokenId); if (!home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'pending home not found' }), { status:404, headers:cors }); // Either the enroller (enrolledBy) or the assigned agent (agentId) can confirm/manage this home. if (home.agentId && who.agentId && home.agentId !== who.agentId && (home.enrolledBy||'') !== who.agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'not your home' }), { status:403, headers:cors }); // Cap check at the moment of going live (pending homes didn't count). const activeCount = await _ntActiveCount(token, who.agentId); const effCap = await _ntEffectiveCap(token, who.agentId); if (home.active !== true && activeCount >= effCap) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'You\'ve reached your '+effCap+'-home limit. Upgrade for more, or remove some to add another.', capReached:true, cap:effCap }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } const upd = { active: fsBool(true), pending: fsBool(false), confirmedAt: fsTimestamp(), needsReview: fsBool(false), reviewReason: fsString('') }; const override = (body.overrideValue!=null) ? Number(String(body.overrideValue).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) : 0; let finalValue = Number(home.value) || 0; if (override && override > 0) { finalValue = Math.round(override); const baseVal = Number(home.value) || finalValue; const hiRatio = (Number(home.high) && baseVal) ? Number(home.high)/baseVal : 1.09; const loRatio = (Number(home.low) && baseVal) ? Number(home.low)/baseVal : 0.91; upd.value = fsInt(finalValue); upd.high = fsInt(Math.round(finalValue*hiRatio)); upd.low = fsInt(Math.round(finalValue*loRatio)); upd.overrideValue = fsInt(finalValue); upd.valueSource = fsString('agent'); // Recompute equity against the corrected value. let equity=null; try { if(home.equityJson) equity=JSON.parse(home.equityJson); } catch(e){} if (equity && equity.estLoanBalance!=null){ equity.equity = Math.max(0, finalValue - Number(equity.estLoanBalance)); equity.equityPct = finalValue ? Math.round(equity.equity/finalValue*100) : null; upd.equityJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(equity)); } else { // Manual-value home (no prior equity): build it now from stored loan data or an entered balance, // so amortized equity works for duplexes/multifamily the same as AVM homes. const _owed = (home.ownerOwed!=null && String(home.ownerOwed)!=='') ? Number(String(home.ownerOwed).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) : null; const _loanAmt = Number(String(home.loanAmount||'').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||0; let bal = null; if (_owed != null) bal = _owed; else if (_loanAmt > 0 && String(home.loanTerm||'') !== ''){ const _term = (String(home.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; let _ratePct; if (home.ownerRate!=null && String(home.ownerRate)!=='') _ratePct = Number(String(home.ownerRate).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); else { let _yr=null; const _st=home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||''; if(_st){ const _sd=/^\d{4}$/.test(String(_st).trim())?new Date(Number(_st),0,1):new Date(_st); if(_sd&&!isNaN(_sd.getTime())) _yr=_sd.getFullYear(); } _ratePct=(_rateForYear(_yr)||0.06)*100; } bal = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, _ratePct, _term, _monthsSince(home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||'')); } if (bal != null){ const eqObj = { estimated:false, estLoanBalance: bal, equity: Math.max(0, finalValue-bal), equityPct: finalValue?Math.round(Math.max(0,finalValue-bal)/finalValue*100):null }; upd.equityJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(eqObj)); } else { // No balance and no loan amount, but we have what they paid — fall back to the same // 20%-down / 30-yr / rate-by-year estimate the AVM path uses. Without this a manual-value // home showed no equity at all, even with a purchase price on file, and the client had // nothing to correct from in their report. try { const _est = estimateEquity(finalValue, home.purchasePrice || '', home.purchaseDate || '', null); if (_est) upd.equityJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(_est)); } catch(e){} } } // Reflect override in the current quarter's history entry. let history=[]; try { history = home.historyJson? JSON.parse(home.historyJson):[]; } catch(e){} if (history.length){ history[history.length-1].value = finalValue; upd.historyJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(history)); } } else { upd.valueSource = fsString('avm'); } try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, upd, Object.keys(upd)); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'confirm write failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } // Point the client's own user doc at their live home. The in-app home dashboard reads this token // from their profile and fetches the (public, token-authed) report with it. Best-effort: if the // client has no portal account this is a harmless no-op and they just keep getting the email. if (home.contactUid){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/'+home.contactUid, { ntHomeToken: fsString(tokenId), ntHomeAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['ntHomeToken','ntHomeAt']); } catch(e){} // Seed the maintenance schedule off the agent's explicit choice at enrollment. Inferring it // from crmStatus meant enrolling before closing the deal silently produced no schedule, and // nothing anywhere said so. if (home.homeDash === true && !home.tasksJson){ try { const _ms = await _ntMaintSettings(token, home.agentId); await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, { tasksJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(ntSeedTasks(_ms, home.purchaseDate))) }, ['tasksJson']); } catch(e){} } // Seed the home file from anything the agent captured at the walkthrough. It was staged on the // contact before this home existed. Apply it ONLY if the address matches — a dead deal's data // must never land on a different house the client later buys. Filter sizes flow into reminders. if (!home.itemsJson){ try { const cDoc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/'+home.contactUid); let intake = null; try { intake = cDoc && cDoc.homeIntake ? JSON.parse(cDoc.homeIntake) : null; } catch(e){} const norm = function(a){ return String(a||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''); }; const homeAddr = norm(home.mailingAddress || home.matchedAddress || ''); const intakeAddr = norm(intake && intake.address); // Match if either address contains the other's street-and-number core (handles "139 Lakewood // Dr" vs "139 Lakewood Dr, Avon Lake, OH 44012"). Empty intake address → don't guess, skip. const matches = intake && intake.items && intake.items.length && intakeAddr && (homeAddr.indexOf(intakeAddr) === 0 || intakeAddr.indexOf(homeAddr) === 0 || homeAddr === intakeAddr); if (matches){ // Push captured filter sizes into the seeded schedule. let tasks = []; try { tasks = JSON.parse((await fsGetDoc(token,'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId)).tasksJson || '[]'); } catch(e){} intake.items.forEach(function(it){ if (it.taskKey && it.spec){ var t = tasks.filter(function(x){return x.key===it.taskKey;})[0]; if(t) t.spec = it.spec; } }); const patch = { itemsJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(intake.items)) }, mask = ['itemsJson']; if (tasks.length){ patch.tasksJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(tasks)); mask.push('tasksJson'); } await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, patch, mask); // Consume it, so it can't seed a second home later. await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/'+home.contactUid, { homeIntake: fsString('') }, ['homeIntake']); } } catch(e){} } } // Send the FIRST home-value report immediately on enroll (welcome), then quarterly after. // Skips if the home has no recipient email or the client already opted out. Best-effort — a send // failure never blocks the enroll from succeeding. let firstSent = false, firstSendReason = ''; try { // Merge the just-written fields onto the home so the email reflects the confirmed value/equity. const merged = Object.assign({}, home, { value: (upd.value && upd.value.integerValue!=null) ? Number(upd.value.integerValue) : (finalValue||Number(home.value)||0), high: (upd.high && upd.high.integerValue!=null) ? Number(upd.high.integerValue) : Number(home.high)||0, low: (upd.low && upd.low.integerValue!=null) ? Number(upd.low.integerValue) : Number(home.low)||0, equityJson: (upd.equityJson && upd.equityJson.stringValue!=null) ? upd.equityJson.stringValue : (home.equityJson||''), historyJson: (upd.historyJson && upd.historyJson.stringValue!=null) ? upd.historyJson.stringValue : (home.historyJson||'') }); if (home.emailsOff === true){ firstSendReason = 'client unsubscribed'; } else if (!(merged.email && String(merged.email).trim())){ firstSendReason = 'no recipient email'; } else { const sres = await _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, merged, tokenId, '', { isFirst: true }); firstSent = !!(sres && sres.ok); firstSendReason = (sres && sres.reason) || ''; // Stamp lastPulledAt so the very next quarterly sweep doesn't immediately re-send. if (firstSent){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, { lastPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), firstReportSentAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['lastPulledAt','firstReportSentAt']); } catch(e){} } } } catch(e){ firstSendReason = 'send error: '+(e&&e.message||'network'); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, token: tokenId, value: finalValue, firstSent: firstSent, firstSendReason: firstSendReason, reportUrl:'https://app.nesttrack.io/report.html?t='+tokenId }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/deals-stamp-members → write memberIds onto every deal from its client card. // Deal visibility is enforced by rules, and rules cannot look up another document on a query // (10-get limit), so the ids have to live ON the deal. The app stamps new deals; this repairs // every existing one in a single pass. Runs with an admin token, so it reaches every folder. async function apiDealsStampMembers(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const teamId = (who && (who.teamId || who.agentId)) || ''; if (!teamId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no team' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'deals', allDescendants:true }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'teamId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:teamId} } }, limit: 2000 }); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const clientCache = {}; let scanned = 0, stamped = 0; for (const r of (rows||[])){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; scanned++; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); const path = r.document.name.split('/documents/')[1]; const owner = (function(){ try { return path.split('/')[1]; } catch(e){ return ''; } })(); let c = null; if (d.clientUid){ if (clientCache[d.clientUid] === undefined){ try { clientCache[d.clientUid] = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/'+d.clientUid); } catch(e){ clientCache[d.clientUid] = null; } } c = clientCache[d.clientUid]; } const ids = []; [owner, d.agentId, d.coAgentId, d.lenderId, c && c.agentId, c && c.coAgentId, c && c.lenderId].forEach(function(v){ if (v && ids.indexOf(v) === -1) ids.push(v); }); if (!ids.length) continue; const before = (d.memberIds || []).slice().sort().join(','); if (before === ids.slice().sort().join(',')) continue; try { await fsPatchFields(token, path, { memberIds: { arrayValue: { values: ids.map(function(v){ return { stringValue: v }; }) } } }, ['memberIds']); stamped++; } catch(e){} } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, scanned:scanned, stamped:stamped }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // The branded listing email. Cadence is entirely the MLS's \u2014 the agent sets Daily or ASAP // in their saved search, we send when we receive. No batching, no scheduling, nothing to // configure twice. // // Rating links carry a token and need no login. Requiring one gets close to zero ratings, // and the whole point of this feature is learning what the buyer actually thinks. function _mlsListingEmailHtml(agent, client, listings, feedToken) { const RATE = 'https://app.tom-b52.workers.dev/r'; const first = (client.firstName || '').trim(); function card(l) { const rate = function (v) { return RATE + '?l=' + encodeURIComponent(l.mlsNumber || l.recordId) + '&c=' + encodeURIComponent(feedToken) + '&v=' + v; }; const specs = [l.beds ? l.beds + ' bd' : '', l.baths ? l.baths + ' ba' : '', l.sqft ? Number(l.sqft).toLocaleString() + ' sqft' : ''] .filter(Boolean).join(' · '); const drop = /decreas/i.test(l.status || ''); const pill = l.status ? '
' + l.status + '
' : ''; return '' + (l.photoUrl ? '' : '') + '
' + pill + '
' + (l.price || '') + '
' + '
' + (l.street || '') + '
' + '
' + (l.cityState || '') + '
' + (specs ? '
' + specs + '
' : '') + '
What do you think?
' + '' + '' + '' + '' + '
Not for meMaybe❤️ Love it
' + 'See all photos on the MLS' + (l.mlsNumber ? '
MLS #' + l.mlsNumber + '
' : '') + '
'; } const n = listings.length; // A bare
has no declared character set, so mail clients guess \u2014 usually Latin-1 \u2014 // and every em dash and curly quote arrives as mojibake. return '' + '' + '' + '
' + '
Hi' + (first ? ' ' + first : '') + ',
' + '
' + (n === 1 ? 'A new home for you' : n + ' new homes for you') + '
' + listings.map(card).join('') + '
' + 'Listing data provided by the MLS.

' + 'Tap Love it or Not for me on any of these and I will see it right away \u2014 ' + 'it helps me send you better matches.

' + (agent.name || 'Your agent') + (agent.phone ? '
' + agent.phone : '') + '
' + '
' + 'Sent through NestTrack. Reply to this email to reach ' + (agent.firstName || 'your agent') + ' directly.' + '
' + '
'; } async function _mlsSendListingEmail(env, token, client, listings) { if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY) return { ok: false, reason: 'no RESEND_API_KEY' }; const to = (client.email || '').trim(); if (!to) return { ok: false, reason: 'client has no email' }; if (client.mlsEmailsOff === true) return { ok: false, reason: 'client opted out' }; if (!listings.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'nothing to send' }; const agent = await _ntResolveAgent(token, client.agentId || ''); const n = listings.length; const subject = n === 1 ? ('New listing: ' + (listings[0].street || 'a home for you')) : (n + ' new listings for you'); const payload = { from: _agentFromField(agent, LISTINGS_DOMAIN), to: [to], subject: subject, html: _mlsListingEmailHtml(agent, client, listings, client.mlsFeedToken || '') }; // Replies go to the agent's real inbox, not a no-reply nobody reads. if (agent && agent.email) payload.reply_to = agent.email; try { const r = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.RESEND_API_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, reason: 'resend ' + r.status }; try { await _logSentEmail(token, client.agentId || '', to, subject, 'mls-listings', _ntHomeFirst(client) || '', client.uid); } catch (e) {} return { ok: true }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'send error: ' + (e && e.message) }; } } // Push when genuinely new listings land. An update to one they already have is not news, // so this only fires on res.added \u2014 a price change already shows on the card. async function _mlsPushListings(env, token, client, listings, added) { if (!added) return 'no new listings'; if (client.mlsEmailsOff === true) return 'client opted out'; const first = listings[0] || {}; const heading = added === 1 ? 'New listing for you' : (added + ' new listings for you'); const body = added === 1 ? [first.price, first.street, (first.cityState || '').split(',')[0]].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') : 'Tap to see them'; // Deep-links straight to the stack. Universal links ship in 1.6, so on a phone with the app // this opens the app rather than the browser; without it, the token view renders on the web. const url = 'https://app.nesttrack.io/#listing=' + encodeURIComponent(first.mlsNumber || first.recordId || '') + '&c=' + encodeURIComponent(client.mlsFeedToken || ''); const ok = await _pushClient(env, client.uid, heading, body, { token: token, url: url }); return ok ? 'pushed' : 'push failed'; } // Age listings out so the stack stays useful. Without this it becomes a junk drawer within // a month \u2014 an active buyer on a daily search collects a couple of hundred cards. // // Rules differ by what the client said, because the rating is the whole signal: // // unrated 10 days \u2014 they never engaged; a fortnight-old listing they ignored is noise // passed 30 days \u2014 hidden from them immediately, but kept so the agent's "passing on // Brecksville" read still has something to work from // maybe 60 days \u2014 a real maybe deserves longer than a shrug // loved kept \u2014 that is their shortlist; only they remove it // // Sold and closed cards follow the unrated rule: informational, and stale quickly. var MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS = { unrated: 10, pass: 30, maybe: 60, love: 0 }; // 0 = never function _mlsExpiryFor(l) { const r = String(l.rating || '').toLowerCase(); if (r === 'love') return 0; if (r === 'maybe') return MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS.maybe; if (r === 'pass') return MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS.pass; return MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS.unrated; } // A listing's clock runs from the last thing that happened to it, not from when it arrived \u2014 // a card they opened last week should not vanish because the email was three weeks ago. function _mlsLastTouch(l) { const stamps = [l.ratedAt, l.lastViewedAt, l.showingRequestedAt, l.lastSeenAt, l.firstSeenAt]; let newest = 0; for (const t of stamps) { if (!t) continue; const ms = (t.seconds ? t.seconds * 1000 : Date.parse(t)) || 0; if (ms > newest) newest = ms; } return newest; } // dryRun reports what would go without touching anything. The cron runs it for real; the // admin route defaults to dry so it can be inspected first. async function _mlsExpireListings(env, token, dryRun) { let scanned = 0, expired = 0, kept = 0; const sample = []; let clients = []; try { clients = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'mlsFeedActive' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: true } } }, limit: 2000 }); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'client query: ' + e.message }; } const now = Date.now(); for (const c of (clients || [])) { if (!c.document || !c.document.fields) continue; const uid = c.document.name.split('/').pop(); let docs = []; try { const url = 'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/users/' + uid + '/listings?pageSize=300'; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); const j = await r.json(); if (!r.ok) continue; docs = j.documents || []; } catch (e) { continue; } for (const doc of docs) { if (!doc.fields) continue; scanned++; const l = digDoc(doc.fields); const days = _mlsExpiryFor(l); if (!days) { kept++; continue; } // loved const touched = _mlsLastTouch(l); if (!touched) { kept++; continue; } // no timestamp \u2014 leave it alone const ageDays = Math.floor((now - touched) / 86400000); if (ageDays < days) { kept++; continue; } expired++; if (sample.length < 12) { sample.push({ uid: uid, mls: doc.name.split('/').pop(), rating: l.rating || 'unrated', ageDays: ageDays, address: l.address || l.street || '' }); } if (!dryRun) { try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/' + doc.name, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); } catch (e) {} } } } return { ok: true, dryRun: !!dryRun, scanned, expired, kept, sample }; } // GET /api/v1/mls-view?c= — a client's listings, no login. // POST /api/v1/mls-view — { c, mls, rating } to record a rating. // // Runs through the worker rather than letting the app read Firestore directly, because a // signed-out lookup by token would need a list rule on /users that rules cannot constrain // to the token — it would have to pass for every user document. This keeps the rules shut. async function apiMlsView(request, env, cors) { const u = new URL(request.url); const tok = String(u.searchParams.get('c') || '').toLowerCase(); const json = function (o, st) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { status: st || 200, headers: cors }); }; if (!tok) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'no token' }, 400); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await _mlsResolveClient(token, 'mls-' + tok + '@x'); if (!client) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'not found' }, 404); if (request.method === 'POST') { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const mls = String(body.mls || ''); const rating = String(body.rating || '').toLowerCase(); if (!mls) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'no listing' }, 400); if (['love', 'maybe', 'pass', ''].indexOf(rating) === -1) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad rating' }, 400); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + mls, { rating: fsString(rating), ratedAt: fsTimestamp(), ratedFrom: fsString('link'), lastActivityAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['rating', 'ratedAt', 'ratedFrom', 'lastActivityAt']); return json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { return json({ ok: false, reason: e.message }, 500); } } // Only what the listings view needs — no email, phone, notes or anything else off the // client record. The token is a link in an inbox, so it gets the minimum. // Straight list of the subcollection. A collectionGroup query on 'listings' filtered by // agentId needed a collection-group index that does not exist, so it came back empty and // the client saw "All caught up" with listings sitting right there in Firestore. let listings = []; try { const url = 'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/users/' + client.uid + '/listings?pageSize=300'; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); const j = await r.json(); if (!r.ok) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'list failed: ' + (j && j.error && j.error.message || r.status) }, 500); for (const doc of (j.documents || [])) { if (!doc.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(doc.fields); d.id = doc.name.split('/').pop(); listings.push(d); } } catch (e) { return json({ ok: false, reason: 'list error: ' + e.message }, 500); } return json({ ok: true, client: { uid: client.uid, firstName: client.firstName || '' }, listings: listings }); } // GET /r?l=&c=&v=love|maybe|pass \u2014 one-click rating from the email. // No login on purpose: requiring one gets close to zero ratings, and knowing what the // buyer thinks is the entire point of this feature. async function apiMlsRate(request, env, cors) { const u = new URL(request.url); const mls = u.searchParams.get('l') || ''; const tok = (u.searchParams.get('c') || '').toLowerCase(); const val = (u.searchParams.get('v') || '').toLowerCase(); const okVal = ['love', 'maybe', 'pass'].indexOf(val) !== -1; const page = function (title, body) { return new Response( '' + '' + title + '' + '' + '
' + body + '
' + '', { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8' } }); }; if (!mls || !tok || !okVal) { return page('Link problem', '
That link looks incomplete.
' + '
Open the app and rate it there instead.
'); } try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await _mlsResolveClient(token, 'mls-' + tok + '@x'); if (!client) return page('Not found', '
We could not match that link.
'); await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + mls, { rating: fsString(val), ratedAt: fsTimestamp(), ratedFrom: fsString('email'), lastActivityAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['rating', 'ratedAt', 'ratedFrom', 'lastActivityAt']); let l = null; try { l = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + mls); } catch (e) {} const said = val === 'love' ? 'Love it' : (val === 'maybe' ? 'Maybe' : 'Not for me'); const tint = val === 'love' ? '#0F7A52' : '#666'; return page('Thanks', '
' + (val === 'love' ? '❤️' : (val === 'maybe' ? '🤔' : '👎')) + '
' + '
You said “' + said + '”
' + (l && l.address ? '
' + l.address + '
' : '') + '
Your agent will see this straight away.
' + 'See all your listings'); } catch (e) { return page('Something went wrong', '
We could not save that just now.
' + '
Try again from the app.
'); } } // POST /api/v1/mls-paste — run the parser against a raw email pasted by the agent. // Gmail rewrites HTML when you forward (strips comments and class attributes, which is // exactly what the parser reads), so forwarding is a useless test. "Show original" gives // the untouched message. Doubles as a recovery path if a real alert ever fails to parse. async function apiMlsPaste(request, env, cors, who) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const raw = String(body.raw || ''); const clientUid = String(body.clientUid || ''); if (!raw) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no raw email' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const html = _mimeHtmlPart(raw); const parsed = parseMlsAlert(html); // Dry run by default — report what was found without writing anything. if (!clientUid) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, isWelcome: parsed.isWelcome, contactId: parsed.contactId, setId: parsed.setId, found: parsed.listings.length, bodyChars: html.length, listings: parsed.listings.map(function (l) { return { mls: l.mlsNumber, price: l.price, address: l.address, beds: l.beds, baths: l.baths, sqft: l.sqft, status: l.status, hasPhoto: !!l.photoUrl, hasLink: !!l.listingUrl }; }) }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + clientUid); if (!client) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'client not found' }), { status:404, headers:cors }); client.uid = clientUid; const res = await _mlsStoreListings(token, client, parsed); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + clientUid, { mlsFeedActive: fsBool(true), mlsFeedLastAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['mlsFeedActive', 'mlsFeedLastAt']); } catch (e) {} return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, added:res.added, updated:res.updated, found:parsed.listings.length }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-reanchor → apply the agent's CURRENT maintenance schedule to homes // they have ALREADY enrolled. Without this, changing the anchor month only ever affected future // enrollments, so an agent who set October watched existing clients keep firing in April and had // no way to correct it. Completed history and per-task overrides are untouched; only nextDue moves. async function apiNestTrackerReanchor(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const agentId = (who && who.agentId) || ''; if (!agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no agent' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const settings = await _ntMaintSettings(token, agentId); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'agentId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:agentId} } }, limit: 500 }); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const base = Date.now(); let homes = 0, moved = 0, turnedOff = 0; for (const r of (rows||[])){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const h = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (h.active === false || h.pending === true || !h.tasksJson) continue; const tok = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); let tasks = []; try { tasks = JSON.parse(h.tasksJson); } catch(e){ continue; } let changed = false; tasks = tasks.filter(function(t){ // Only the agent-configurable big-ticket tasks follow the schedule. DIY consumables // (filter, batteries) and the anniversary tasks are timed by other rules entirely. if (!MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS[t.key] || t.anniversary) return true; const s = settings[t.key]; if (!s) return true; if (s.off === true){ turnedOff++; changed = true; return false; } // agent switched it off const every = (Number(s.freqDays) > 0) ? Number(s.freqDays) : (Number(t.everyDays)||182); const month = (s.month != null && s.month >= 0 && s.month <= 11) ? Number(s.month) : null; const next = (month != null) ? _nextAnchoredMonth(base, month, every) : _nextSeasonal(base); if (next !== t.nextDue){ t.nextDue = next; t.everyDays = every; moved++; changed = true; } return true; }); if (changed){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok, { tasksJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(tasks)) }, ['tasksJson']); homes++; } catch(e){} } } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, homes:homes, moved:moved, turnedOff:turnedOff }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-remove → deactivate a home (frees a cap slot; doc kept for history). async function apiNestTrackerRemove(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch(e){ body = {}; } const tok = String(body.token || '').trim(); if (!tok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'missing token' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + tok); if (!home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'home not found' }), { status: 404, headers: cors }); // Either the enroller (enrolledBy) or the assigned agent (agentId) can manage this home. if (home.agentId && who.agentId && home.agentId !== who.agentId && (home.enrolledBy||'') !== who.agentId) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'not your home' }), { status: 403, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/nesttracker-dashboard → agent-scoped monitoring for the marketing tile. // Returns real, tracked metrics only (no fabricated numbers): counts, cap usage, the due/processed // queue readout, engagement (check-ins), and a per-home list. Reads only — no ATTOM calls. async function apiNestTrackerDashboard(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'agentId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue: who.agentId||'' } } }, limit: 500 }); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const now = Date.now(); const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); const dueMs = 89*24*60*60*1000; let active=0, pending=0, dueToday=0, processedToday=0, checkins=0, overridden=0, warm=0, lockedCount=0; const homes = []; if (Array.isArray(rows)) for (const r of rows){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const h = digDoc(r.document.fields); const tokenId = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const isActive = h.active === true; const isPending = h.active === false && h.pending === true; if (h.active === false && !isPending) continue; // skip removed if (isActive) active++; if (isPending) pending++; // Pending homes are normally mid-enroll (previewed, not confirmed) and hidden. BUT a home // flagged needsReview (auto-enrolled with no/low value) MUST be listed so the agent can act // on it — otherwise it's invisible with no way to review. Show those; hide plain pending. if (isPending && h.needsReview !== true) continue; const isLocked = h.locked === true; if (isLocked) lockedCount++; const lastMs = h.lastPulledAt ? Date.parse(h.lastPulledAt) : 0; if (isActive && !isLocked && (!lastMs || (now-lastMs) >= dueMs)) dueToday++; if (h.lastPulledAt && String(h.lastPulledAt).slice(0,10) === todayStr) processedToday++; if (h.lastCheckinAt) checkins++; if (h.valueSource === 'agent' || (h.overrideValue!=null && String(h.overrideValue)!=='')) overridden++; homes.push({ token: tokenId, name: ((h.firstName||'')+' '+(h.lastName||'')).trim() || (h.email||'Client'), email: h.email||'', uid: h.contactUid||'', address: h.mailingAddress || h.matchedAddress || '', value: Number(h.value)||0, status: isPending ? 'pending' : (isLocked ? 'locked' : 'active'), locked: isLocked, overridden: (h.valueSource==='agent' || (h.overrideValue!=null && String(h.overrideValue)!=='')), homeDash: (h.homeDash !== false), needsReview: (h.needsReview === true), reviewReason: h.reviewReason || '', lowConfidence: (h.lowConfidence === true), confidence: Number(h.confidence)||0, low: Number(h.low)||0, high: Number(h.high)||0, lastPulledAt: h.lastPulledAt||'', lastCheckinAt: h.lastCheckinAt||'', lastIntent: h.lastIntent||'', logCount: (function(){ try { return (JSON.parse(h.logJson||'[]')||[]).length; } catch(e){ return 0; } })(), valuePaused: (h.emailsOff === true), lastLogAt: (function(){ try { var L=JSON.parse(h.logJson||'[]')||[]; return (L[0]&&L[0].ts)||''; } catch(e){ return ''; } })(), lastViewedAt: h.lastViewedAt||'', viewCount: Number(h.viewCount)||0, lastSentAt: h.lastPulledAt || '', nextDueAt: (function(){ try { var lp = h.lastPulledAt; var ms = lp && lp.seconds ? lp.seconds*1000 : (lp ? Stores the request + notifies the platform owner. Pricing/structure TBD. async function apiNestTrackerRequestCap(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body={}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch(e){} const fields = { agentId: fsString(who.agentId||''), agentEmail: fsString(who.email || ''), currentCap: fsInt(NESTTRACKER_CAP), note: fsString(String(body.note||'').slice(0,500)), status: fsString('requested'), requestedAt: fsTimestamp() }; try { await firestoreWrite(token, 'nesttracker_cap_requests/' + (who.agentId||('anon_'+Date.now())), fields); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'could not save request: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } // Best-effort notify the platform owner (non-blocking). try { if (env.RESEND_API_KEY && env.OWNER_EMAIL){ await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method:'POST', headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+env.RESEND_API_KEY,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({ from:'NestTracker ', to:[env.OWNER_EMAIL], subject:'NestTracker: home-cap upgrade request', html:'

' }) }); } } catch(e){} return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, status:'requested' }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-bulk-remove → deactivate multiple homes at once (agent-scoped), then // re-reconcile locks so freeing slots can unlock previously-locked homes. async function apiNestTrackerBulkRemove(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body={}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch(e){} const tokens = Array.isArray(body.tokens) ? body.tokens.slice(0,200) : []; if (!tokens.length) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no tokens' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); let removed = 0; const removedUids = []; for (const t of tokens){ const tok = String(t||'').trim(); if (!tok) continue; try { const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok); if (!home) continue; if (home.agentId && who.agentId && home.agentId !== who.agentId && (home.enrolledBy||'') !== who.agentId) continue; // only your own (assigned agent or enroller) await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok, { active: fsBool(false), pending: fsBool(false), needsReview: fsBool(false), reviewReason: fsString(''), deactivatedAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['active','pending','needsReview','reviewReason','deactivatedAt']); removed++; if (home.contactUid) removedUids.push(home.contactUid); } catch(e){} } try { await _ntReconcileAgentLocks(token, who.agentId, env); } catch(e){} // freeing slots may unlock others return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, removed:removed, removedUids:removedUids }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // ── Live mortgage rates (Freddie Mac PMMS via FRED), cached once/day in Firestore. ── // Needs env.FRED_API_KEY (free: https://fredaccount.stlouisfed.org/apikeys). Returns nulls // gracefully if the key is missing so the report never breaks. async function getLiveRates(env, token){ const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); try { const cached = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_meta/rates'); if (cached && cached.day === todayStr && cached.rate30) { return { rate30: Number(cached.rate30)||null, rate15: Number(cached.rate15)||null, asOf: cached.asOf||todayStr }; } } catch(e){} const key = (env.FRED_API_KEY||'').trim(); if (!key) return { rate30:null, rate15:null, asOf:null }; async function series(id){ try { const u = 'https://api.stlouisfed.org/fred/series/observations?series_id='+id+'&api_key='+key+'&file_type=json&sort_order=desc&limit=1'; const r = await fetch(u); if(!r.ok) return null; const j = await r.json(); const o = j && j.observations && j.observations[0]; if (o && o.value && o.value!=='.') return { v:Number(o.value), d:o.date }; } catch(e){} return null; } const s30 = await series('MORTGAGE30US'); const s15 = await series('MORTGAGE15US'); const out = { rate30: s30? s30.v : null, rate15: s15? s15.v : null, asOf: (s30&&s30.d)||(s15&&s15.d)||todayStr }; try { await firestoreWrite(token, 'nesttracker_meta/rates', { day: fsString(todayStr), rate30: fsString(out.rate30!=null?String(out.rate30):''), rate15: fsString(out.rate15!=null?String(out.rate15):''), asOf: fsString(out.asOf||''), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} return out; } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-checkin?t=TOKEN → PUBLIC. The homeowner tapped a check-in button // (or saved their rate). Persists engagement, flags the linked contact warm, alerts the agent. async function apiNestTrackerCheckin(request, env, cors){ let tok=''; try { tok=(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('t')||'').trim(); } catch(e){} let body={}; try { body=await request.json(); } catch(e){} if(!tok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing token'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token,'nesttracker_homes/'+tok); if(!home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); const intent = String(body.intent||'').slice(0,120); const ownerRate = (body.ownerRate!=null) ? Writes // are fire-and-forget: a failed log must never break or delay a send. async function _logSentEmail(token, agentId, to, subject, type, clientName, clientUid){ try { if (!token || !subject) return; const _to = Array.isArray(to) ? to.filter(Boolean).join(', ') : String(to||''); if (!_to) return; const _id = 'e_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8); const _sub = String(subject).slice(0,200); // The agent's own log — one list across every client, powers Email → Sent. if (agentId){ await firestoreWrite(token, 'agents/'+agentId+'/sentEmails/'+_id, { to: fsString(_to), subject: fsString(_sub), type: fsString(type||'general'), clientName: fsString(String(clientName||'')), clientUid: fsString(String(clientUid||'')), source: fsString('worker'), sentAt: fsTimestamp() }); } // And the client's own card, so opening a contact shows what they have received. // Same collection the app writes to, same field names. if (clientUid){ await firestoreWrite(token, 'users/'+clientUid+'/emailLog/'+_id, { subject: fsString(_sub), type: fsString(type||'general'), agentId: fsString(String(agentId||'')), source: fsString('worker'), sentAt: fsTimestamp() }); } } catch(e){ /* logging is never allowed to matter */ } } async function _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, home, tokenId, toOverride, opts){ opts = opts || {}; if(!home) return { ok:false, reason:'no home' }; if(!env.RESEND_API_KEY) return { ok:false, reason:'no RESEND_API_KEY' }; const to = String(toOverride || home.email || '').trim(); if(!to) return { ok:false, reason:'no recipient email on this home' }; let equity=null, history=[]; try { if(home.equityJson) equity=JSON.parse(home.equityJson); } catch(e){} try { history = home.historyJson ? Number(equity.estLoanBalance) : null; let equityAmt = (equity && equity.equity!=null) ? Number(equity.equity) : (owed!=null?Math.max(0,value-owed):null); let equityPct = (equity && equity.equityPct!=null) ? Number(equity.equityPct) : (value&&equityAmt!=null?Math.round(equityAmt/value*100):null); if(home.ownerOwed!=null && String(home.ownerOwed)!==''){ const oo=Number(String(home.ownerOwed).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); if(!isNaN(oo)){ owed=oo; equityAmt=Math.max(0,value-oo); equityPct=value?Math.round(equityAmt/value*100):null; } } let qtrDelta=0; if(Array.isArray(history)&&history.length>=2){ const a=Number(history[history.length-2].value)||0,b=Number(history[history.length-1].value)||0; if(a>0) qtrDelta=((b-a)/a)*100; } else if(home.monthlyChangePct) qtrDelta=Number(home.monthlyChangePct)||0; const agent = await _ntResolveAgent(token, home.agentId); const lender = await _ntResolveLender(token, home.lenderId); // co-brand the finance moments when a lender is assigned // The growth split and the hook. When equityJson is empty, `equity` stays null // and eqObj is rebuilt from the loan inputs just above — referencing `equity` here threw // "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'estLoanBalance')" and killed the whole send. const eqEff = Object.assign({}, eqObj, { estLoanBalance: (owed != null ? owed : eqObj.estLoanBalance), equity: (equityAmt != null ? equityAmt : eqObj.equity), equityPct: (equityPct != null ? equityPct : eqObj.equityPct) }); try { insight = equityInsight(eqEff, home, history, value, _rates.rate30); } catch(e){ insight = null; } } // Exactly one review per ownership year, on the first quarterly at or after the anniversary. A // fixed month window would miss the year entirely whenever the sends fell either side of it. const _ownYear = insight ? Math.floor(insight.monthsOwned / 12) : 0; const _isPreview = !!toOverride; const _force = String(opts.force || ''); // 'review' | 'pmi' — preview only, never stamps const reviewDue = (_force === 'review') || (!opts.isFirst && !_isPreview && _ownYear >= 1 && Number(home.reviewSentFor||0) < _ownYear); // Crossing 20% equity is real money on the table every month it goes unnoticed. Sends a home's teaser now (for testing). // body: { t|token: homeToken, to?: override recipient for a safe preview }. async function apiNestTrackerSendTeaser(request, env, cors, who){ let body={}; try { body=await request.json(); } catch(e){} const tokenId = String(body.t || body.token || '').trim(); if(!tokenId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing home token'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token,'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId); if(!home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'home not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); if(who && home.agentId && who.agentId && home.agentId !== who.agentId && (home.enrolledBy||'') !== who.agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not your home'}),{status:403,headers:cors}); const res = await _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, home, tokenId, (body.to||'').trim(), { force: (body.force||'') }); return new Response(JSON.stringify(res),{status: res.ok?200:400, headers:cors}); } // ── PROBE: validate the ZIP→geoIdV4→Sales Trend V4 chain (Model B) before wiring it live. ── // Old geoids (ZI44140) DON'T work with V4 — it needs the 32-char geoIdV4 hash. This: (1) converts // ZI{zip} to geoIdV4 via legacyLookup, (2) calls Sales Trend V4, (3) shows the median-price series // + the quarterly drift % we'd apply. GET ?zip=44140 async function apiNestTrackerMarketTest(request, env, cors){ const key = env.ATTOM_API_KEY; if (!key) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'ATTOM_API_KEY not set' }), { status:500, headers:cors }); const u = new URL(request.url); const zip = (u.searchParams.get('zip')||'').trim(); if (!/^\d{5}$/.test(zip)) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'pass ?zip=44140' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const H = { headers:{ 'apikey':key, 'accept':'application/json' } }; const out = { zip, oldGeoId:'ZI'+zip, geoIdV4:null, lookup:null, trend:null }; // 1) Resolve ZIP → geoIdV4 hash via the authorized /v4/location/lookup. try { const lurl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/location/lookup?geographyTypeAbbreviation=ZI&name=' + encodeURIComponent(zip); const lr = await fetch(lurl, H); const lt = await lr.text(); out.lookup = { status: lr.status, bodySnippet: lt.slice(0,300) }; let lj=null; try { lj=JSON.parse(lt); } catch(e){} if (lj && Array.isArray(lj.geographies) && lj.geographies.length){ let g = lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI' && String(x.geographyName)===zip; }) || lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI'; }) || lj.geographies[0]; out.geoIdV4 = (g && g.geoIdV4) || null; } } catch(e){ out.lookup = { error: e.message }; } if (!out.geoIdV4) { out.ok=false; out.reason='could not resolve geoIdV4 for this ZIP'; return new Response(JSON.stringify(out,null,2),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // 2) Sales Trend V4 with the hash — quarterly, last 3 years. const nowY = new Date().getUTCFullYear(); try { const turl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/transaction/salestrend?geoIdV4=' + encodeURIComponent(out.geoIdV4) + '&interval=quarterly&startyear=' + (nowY-3) + '&endyear=' + nowY; const tr = await fetch(turl, H); const tt = await tr.text(); let tj=null; try { tj=JSON.parse(tt); } catch(e){} const series = _ntParseTrendSeries(tj); out.trend = { status: tr.status, points: series.length, series: series.slice(-10), drift: _ntDriftFromSeries(series) }; if (!series.length) out.trend.bodySnippet = tt.slice(0,400); } catch(e){ out.trend = { error: e.message }; } out.ok = !!(out.trend && out.trend.points); return new Response(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // Parse ATTOM salesTrends JSON → [{q, med, count}] sorted oldest→newest. function _ntParseTrendSeries(tj){ const series = []; if (tj && Array.isArray(tj.salesTrends)){ for (const s of tj.salesTrends){ const st = s.salesTrend || {}; const dr = s.dateRange || {}; const med = Number(st.medSalePrice)||0; if (dr.start && med) series.push({ q: dr.start, med: med, count: Number(st.homeSaleCount)||0 }); } } // sort by year then quarter (start like "Q1-2024") series.sort(function(a,b){ const pa=_ntQKey(a.q), pb=_ntQKey(b.q); return pa-pb; }); return series; } function _ntQKey(qstr){ const m=String(qstr).match(/Q(\d)-(\d{4})/); if(!m) return 0; return parseInt(m[2],10)*4 + parseInt(m[1],10); } // From a quarterly median-price series, compute a SMOOTHED quarterly drift %. // Small ZIPs are noisy quarter-to-quarter (mix of which homes sold), so we compare the trailing // 4-quarter average against the prior 4-quarter average — a year-over-year on averaged data — // then convert to a per-quarter geometric rate. Falls back to a simple span if <8 quarters. function _ntDriftFromSeries(series){ const s = (Array.isArray(series) ? series : []).filter(function(p){ return p && p.med>0; }); if (s.length < 2) return null; const avg = function(arr){ return arr.reduce(function(t,p){ return t+p.med; },0) / arr.length; }; if (s.length < 8){ const a=s[0], b=s[s.length-1], span=s.length-1; if (!a.med) return null; const tot=(b.med-a.med)/a.med, pq=Math.pow(1+tot,1/span)-1; return { method:'span', latestQ:b.q, priorQ:a.q, spanQuarters:span, perQuarterPct:Math.round(pq*10000)/100, annualizedPct:Math.round((Math.pow(1+pq,4)-1)*10000)/100, perQuarterFraction:pq }; } const recent = s.slice(-4), prior = s.slice(-8,-4); const rAvg = avg(recent), pAvg = avg(prior); if (!pAvg) return null; const yoy = (rAvg - pAvg) / pAvg; const perQuarter = Math.pow(1 + yoy, 1/4) - 1; return { method:'4v4', latestQ:s[s.length-1].q, recentAvg:Math.round(rAvg), priorAvg:Math.round(pAvg), yoyPct:Math.round(yoy*10000)/100, perQuarterPct:Math.round(perQuarter*10000)/100, annualizedPct:Math.round((Math.pow(1+perQuarter,4)-1)*10000)/100, perQuarterFraction:perQuarter }; } // ═══════════════════ NESTTRACKER — quarterly value drift (Step 5) ═══════════════════ // Each quarter every enrolled home's value drifts by the real market change since the last // pull, then the teaser email fires automatically (the agent pre-approved at enroll). The // SOURCE of the change % is swappable via env.NESTTRACKER_RATE_SOURCE: // 'avm' (default, in use now) — re-pull ATTOM's AVM per home for a fresh % (~$0.50/home/qtr) // 'trend' (future) — one ZIP-level appreciation % per area, reused across homes // (needs ATTOM's Sales-Trend package; not on the plan today) // Swapping later is a one-function change — the drift math below never changes. async function _ntAvmRate(env, home){ const parts = { street: home.street||'', city: home.city||'', state: home.state||'', zip: home.zip||'' }; const v = await getHomeValue(env, parts); if (!v.ok) return { ok:false, reason: v.reason || 'AVM re-pull failed' }; const refAvm = Number(home.refAvm) || Number(home.value) || v.value; const pct = refAvm ? (Number(v.value) - refAvm) / refAvm : 0; return { ok:true, newAvm: Number(v.value), pct: pct, confidence: v.confidence, monthlyChgPct: v.monthlyChangePct, high: v.high, low: v.low }; } async function _ntTrendRate(env, home){ // MODEL B: drift by the home's ZIP-level market appreciation (ATTOM Sales Trend V4). // One trend call per ZIP per quarter is CACHED and shared by every home in that ZIP. const zip = String(home.zip||'').trim(); if (!/^\d{5}$/.test(zip)) return { ok:false, reason:'no ZIP on home' }; const fsToken = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const pct = await _ntZipTrendPct(env, fsToken, zip); if (pct == null) return { ok:false, reason:'no trend for ZIP '+zip }; // Trend gives a % only; the "newAvm" for the ref chain stays the prior refAvm drifted by pct // so the baseline carry-forward still works without a per-home AVM pull. const refAvm = Number(home.refAvm) || Number(home.value) || 0; const newAvm = Math.round(refAvm * (1 + pct)); return { ok:true, newAvm: newAvm, pct: pct, confidence: Number(home.confidence)||null, monthlyChgPct: null, source:'trend' }; } // Cached ZIP → geoIdV4 hash (one-time lookup per ZIP, stored forever in nesttracker_geoids/{zip}). async function _ntZipGeoId(env, fsToken, zip){ try { const c = await fsGetDoc(fsToken, 'nesttracker_geoids/'+zip); if (c && c.geoIdV4) return c.geoIdV4; } catch(e){} try { const lurl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/location/lookup?geographyTypeAbbreviation=ZI&name=' + encodeURIComponent(zip); const lr = await fetch(lurl, { headers:{ 'apikey':env.ATTOM_API_KEY, 'accept':'application/json' } }); const lj = await lr.json(); let hash = null; if (lj && Array.isArray(lj.geographies) && lj.geographies.length){ const g = lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI' && String(x.geographyName)===zip; }) || lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI'; }) || lj.geographies[0]; hash = (g && g.geoIdV4) || null; } if (hash){ try { await firestoreWrite(fsToken, 'nesttracker_geoids/'+zip, { geoIdV4: fsString(hash), zip: fsString(zip), resolvedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} } return hash; } catch(e){ return null; } } // Cached per-ZIP quarterly drift fraction (one Sales Trend V4 call per ZIP per quarter, shared // by all homes in that ZIP). Stored in nesttracker_trends/{zip}_{quarter}. async function _ntZipTrendPct(env, fsToken, zip){ const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); const cacheId = 'nesttracker_trends/' + zip + '_' + q; // If ANY doc exists for this zip+quarter, trust it and never re-pull — even a "no data" marker. let cached = null; try { cached = await fsGetDoc(fsToken, cacheId); } catch(e){} if (cached) return (cached.pct != null && cached.pct !== '') ? Number(cached.pct) : null; const noData = function(reason){ try { firestoreWrite(fsToken, cacheId, { noData: fsBool(true), zip: fsString(zip), quarter: fsString(q), reason: fsString(reason), computedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} return null; }; const geo = await _ntZipGeoId(env, fsToken, zip); if (!geo) return noData('no geoIdV4 for ZIP'); const nowY = new Date().getUTCFullYear(); try { const turl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/transaction/salestrend?geoIdV4=' + encodeURIComponent(geo) + '&interval=quarterly&startyear=' + (nowY-3) + '&endyear=' + nowY; const tr = await fetch(turl, { headers:{ 'apikey':env.ATTOM_API_KEY, 'accept':'application/json' } }); const tj = await tr.json(); const series = _ntParseTrendSeries(tj); const drift = _ntDriftFromSeries(series); if (!drift || drift.perQuarterFraction == null) return noData('no usable trend series'); // Cap a single quarter's ZIP drift at +/-6% to guard against a thin/noisy quarter. let pct = drift.perQuarterFraction; if (pct > 0.06) pct = 0.06; if (pct < -0.06) pct = -0.06; try { await firestoreWrite(fsToken, cacheId, { pct: fsDouble(pct), annualizedPct: fsDouble(drift.annualizedPct||0), zip: fsString(zip), quarter: fsString(q), latestQ: fsString(drift.latestQ||''), computedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} return pct; } catch(e){ return noData('fetch error'); } } // Read-only "your area" stat for the report — reads the cached ZIP trend for the CURRENT quarter // only (never triggers a pull, so report views stay $0). Returns null if not cached yet. async function _ntZipTrendStat(fsToken, zip){ if (!/^\d{5}$/.test(String(zip||''))) return null; const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); try { const c = await fsGetDoc(fsToken, 'nesttracker_trends/' + zip + '_' + q); if (c && c.annualizedPct != null) return { annualizedPct: Number(c.annualizedPct), latestQ: c.latestQ || '', zip: zip }; } catch(e){} return null; } async function _ntGetQuarterlyRate(env, home){ const force = String(env.NESTTRACKER_RATE_SOURCE || '').toLowerCase(); // '', 'avm', 'trend' const isOverridden = (home.valueSource === 'agent') || (home.overrideValue != null && String(home.overrideValue) !== ''); // Forced modes (testing / manual override of the default behavior). if (force === 'avm') return await _ntAvmRate(env, home); if (force === 'trend'){ const t = await _ntTrendRate(env, home); return t.ok ? t : await _ntAvmRate(env, home); } // DEFAULT (hybrid): // • Overridden homes → drift the agent's corrected value by the ZIP trend. Re-pulling ATTOM // would just return the same value the agent already rejected, so we never pull for these. // If the ZIP has no usable trend, hold the value (skip this quarter) rather than guess. // • Normal homes → re-pull the AVM for a fresh, per-property number (most accurate). if (isOverridden){ const t = await _ntTrendRate(env, home); if (t.ok) return t; return { ok:false, reason:'overridden home, no ZIP trend this quarter — holding value' }; } return await _ntAvmRate(env, home); } // Apply the quarter's drift to ONE home, update the stored snapshot + history, then auto-send the teaser. async function _ntQuarterlyUpdateHome(env, token, home, tokenId, opts){ opts = opts || {}; if (home.active === false) return { ok:false, skipped:'inactive' }; if (home.locked === true) return { ok:false, skipped:'locked (over cap / unpaid)' }; // paused until payment clears // Value-unsubscribed → stop pulling. No email goes out, so a fresh ATTOM valuation would be paid // for and seen by no one. The card shows "value paused — unsubscribed" (via emailsOff) so the // stale number never silently misleads the agent. A forced/preview send (toOverride) still runs. if (home.emailsOff === true && !opts.toOverride){ return { ok:false, skipped:'value paused (client unsubscribed)' }; } const rate = await _ntGetQuarterlyRate(env, home); if (!rate.ok) return { ok:false, skipped:'rate unavailable: ' + (rate.reason||'') }; // don't corrupt value; retry next quarter const baseline = Number(home.value) || rate.newAvm || 0; if (!baseline) return { ok:false, skipped:'no baseline value' }; let newValue = Math.round(baseline * (1 + (rate.pct||0))); if (!(newValue > 0)) newValue = baseline; // Preserve the original high/low ratio around the drifted value. const hiRatio = (Number(home.high) && baseline) ? Number(home.high)/baseline : 1.09; const loRatio = (Number(home.low) && baseline) ? Number(home.low)/baseline : 0.91; const newHigh = Math.round(newValue * hiRatio); const newLow = Math.round(newValue * loRatio); // Recompute equity from the new value + amortized loan paydown. let equity=null; try { if(home.equityJson) equity=JSON.parse(home.equityJson); } catch(e){} let owed = null; // Loan-data path: re-amortize the balance to TODAY so equity grows from paydown too (not just value). // Anchor: a manually-entered ownerOwed (homeowner/agent edit) resets the balance + its date; from that // point we keep paying down. Otherwise amortize from the original loanStart. const _loanAmt = Number(String(home.loanAmount||'').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||0; const _hasLoan = _loanAmt > 0 && String(home.loanTerm||'') !== ''; const _manualOwed = (home.ownerOwed!=null && String(home.ownerOwed)!=='') ? Number(String(home.ownerOwed).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) : null; if (_hasLoan){ const term = (String(home.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; let ratePct; if (home.ownerRate!=null && String(home.ownerRate)!=='') ratePct = Number(String(home.ownerRate).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); else { let yr=null; const st=home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||''; if(st){ const sd=/^\d{4}$/.test(String(st).trim())?new Date(Number(st),0,1):new Date(st); if(sd&&!isNaN(sd.getTime())) yr=sd.getFullYear(); } ratePct=(_rateForYear(yr)||0.06)*100; } if (_manualOwed != null && home.ownerOwedAt){ // Balance was hand-set on ownerOwedAt → amortize the remaining balance forward from that date. const monthsSinceAnchor = _monthsSince(home.ownerOwedAt); // Derive months already paid at the anchor from the anchor balance, then add elapsed months. // Simpler + robust: treat the anchor balance as a fresh loan of (remaining term) and pay it down. const monthsPaidTotal = _monthsSince(home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||''); owed = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, ratePct, term, monthsPaidTotal); // If the manual balance differs from the pure-schedule balance, honor the manual anchor by // shifting: keep the manual value and subtract this quarter's principal delta. const prevSched = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, ratePct, term, Math.max(0, monthsPaidTotal-3)); const principalThisQ = Math.max(0, prevSched - owed); owed = Math.max(0, Math.round(_manualOwed - principalThisQ)); } else { const monthsPaid = _monthsSince(home.loanStart || home.purchaseDate || ''); owed = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, ratePct, term, monthsPaid); } } else if (_manualOwed != null) { owed = _manualOwed; // flat balance, no loan schedule } else if (equity && equity.estLoanBalance!=null) { owed = Number(equity.estLoanBalance); // legacy estimate, carried flat } let newEquityJson = home.equityJson || ''; if (!equity && owed!=null) equity = {}; if (equity){ if (owed!=null){ equity.estLoanBalance = owed; equity.equity = Math.max(0, newValue - owed); equity.equityPct = newValue ? Math.round(equity.equity/newValue*100) : null; if(_hasLoan) equity.estimated=false; } newEquityJson = JSON.stringify(equity); } const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); let history=[]; try { history = home.historyJson ? JSON.parse(home.historyJson) : []; } catch(e){} history = history.filter(function(h){ return h && h.q !== q; }); history.push({ q:q, value:newValue, ts:new Date().toISOString() }); if (history.length>24) history = history.slice(history.length-24); const historyStr = JSON.stringify(history); const upd = { value: fsInt(newValue), high: fsInt(newHigh), low: fsInt(newLow), refAvm: fsInt(rate.newAvm!=null ? rate.newAvm : (Number(home.refAvm)||newValue)), confidence: fsInt(rate.confidence!=null ? rate.confidence : (Number(home.confidence)||0)), monthlyChangePct: fsString(rate.monthlyChgPct==null ? Date.parse(home.lastViewedAt) : 0; const compsMs = home.compsPulledAt ? Date.parse(home.compsPulledAt) : 0; const openedRecently = viewedMs && (now - viewedMs) <= 120*24*60*60*1000; const compsStale = !compsMs || (now - compsMs) >= 182*24*60*60*1000; // ~6 months if (openedRecently && compsStale){ const parts = { street: home.street||'', city: home.city||'', state: home.state||'', zip: home.zip||'' }; const c = await attomGetComps(env, parts); if (c && c.ok && Array.isArray(c.comps)){ upd.compsJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(c.comps)); upd.compsPulledAt = fsTimestamp(); } } } catch(e){ /* comps refresh is best-effort; never block the value update */ } try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, upd, Object.keys(upd)); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, skipped:'write failed: '+e.message }; } // Fully-auto teaser (agent pre-approved at enroll). Send off the freshly-merged snapshot. // Client-unsubscribed homes still refresh their value above (keeps the agent's dashboard live), // but no email goes out — the relationship/contact stays intact, only the emails stop. let sent=false, sendReason=''; if (home.emailsOff === true){ sendReason='client unsubscribed'; } else if (!opts.noSend){ const merged = Object.assign({}, home, { value:newValue, high:newHigh, low:newLow, equityJson:newEquityJson, historyJson:historyStr, monthlyChangePct:(rate.monthlyChgPct==null?'':String(rate.monthlyChgPct)) }); const sres = await _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, merged, tokenId, opts.toOverride||''); sent = !!(sres && sres.ok); sendReason = (sres && sres.reason) || ''; } return { ok:true, newValue:newValue, pct: rate.pct, sent:sent, sendReason:sendReason }; } // Sweep due homes (anniversary-based: ~90 days since last pull). Self-gates — once processed, // lastPulledAt updates so a home won't be due again for a quarter. Capped per run to pace ATTOM/Resend. async function nesttrackerQuarterlySweep(token, env, opts){ opts = opts || {}; const cap = opts.cap || NESTTRACKER_SWEEP_CAP; const dueMs = 89*24*60*60*1000; let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'active'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{booleanValue:true} } }, limit: 400 }); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, error:'query failed: '+e.message }; } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return { ok:false, error:'query returned non-array' }; const now = Date.now(); const out = { ok:true, scanned:0, due:0, processed:0, sent:0, results:[] }; const seenAgents = {}; // refresh each agent's block/payment status at most once per run for (const r of rows){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; out.scanned++; const home = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (home.active === false) continue; const tokenId = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const lastMs = home.lastPulledAt ? Date.parse(home.lastPulledAt) : (home.enrolledAt ? Date.parse(home.enrolledAt) : 0); const isDue = opts.force || !lastMs || (now - lastMs) >= dueMs; if (!isDue) continue; out.due++; // First time we see this agent this run, sync their block/payment status from Stripe and reconcile // locks. Everyone else is capped at 40 by enrollment and can't be over — skip. try { const _a = await _ntResolveAgent(token, _aid); const _ad = (_a && _a._doc) || {}; if (_ad.ntBlockSubId || _ad.ntBlockCustomerId){ await _ntRefreshBlockStatus(token, env, _aid, { uid: _a._uid || '', email: _ad.email || '' }); // re-read this home in case the reconcile just locked it try { const fresh = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId); if (fresh && fresh.locked === true){ out.results.push({ token: tokenId, ok:false, skipped:'locked (over cap / unpaid)' }); continue; } } catch(e){} } } catch(e){} } else if (home.locked === true){ out.results.push({ token: tokenId, ok:false, skipped:'locked (over cap / unpaid)' }); continue; } // Ensure this ZIP's trend is cached for the quarter — powers the report's "your area" stat and // (for overridden homes) the drift. Reads Firestore only — // ZERO AVM pulls, so opening/refreshing the report costs nothing. async function apiNestTrackerReport(request, env, cors){ let tok = ''; try { tok = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('t') || '').trim(); } catch(e){} if (!tok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'missing token' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + tok); if (!home || (home.active === false && home.pending !== true)) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'report not found' }), { status:404, headers:cors }); // Stamp the view (engagement signal + gates the comps refresh). } async function apiHomebotEnroll(request, env, cors, who) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {};
