Date.parse(d.createdAt) || 0 : 0) }); } out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs; }); let sinceMs = 0; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const s = u.searchParams.get('since'); if (s) sinceMs = /^\d+$/.test(s) ? parseInt(s, 10) : (Date.parse(s) || 0); } catch (e) {} const filtered = sinceMs ? out.filter(function (d) { return d.createdAtMs > sinceMs; }) : out; return new Response(JSON.stringify(filtered.slice(0, limit)), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // Log a smart-plan "Zapier step reached" event for the polling trigger above. // Called from spFireStep when a step's channel is 'zapier' with no webhookUrl. // GET /api/v1/zapier-triggers → list of this agent's Zapier-step Trigger IDs + // labels, so Zapier can show them as a dynamic dropdown when building a Zap. async function apiListZapierTriggers(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); let rows; try { const _listUrl = 'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/agents/' + who.agentId + '/smartPlans?pageSize=300'; const _resp = await fetch(_listUrl, { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); const _j = await _resp.json(); rows = (_j && _j.documents) ? _j.documents : []; } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify([]), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } const out = []; if (Array.isArray(rows)) { for (const doc of rows) { if (!doc.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(doc.fields); const planName = d.name || 'Plan'; let steps = d.steps; if (!Array.isArray(steps)) continue; for (const st of steps) { if (!st || typeof st !== 'object') continue; const ch = st.channel || ''; if ((ch === 'zapier' || ch === 'text') && st.triggerId) { out.push({ id: st.triggerId, triggerId: st.triggerId, label: (st.content || 'Zapier step') + ' — ' + planName, stepLabel: st.content || '', planName: planName }); } } } } return new Response(JSON.stringify(out), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } async function logSmartPlanEvent(token, c, plan, step) { try { const agentId = c.smartPlanOwner; if (!agentId) { return; } // no owner → skip; never default to a person const evId = 'zev_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7); const fields = { agentId: fsString(agentId), planName: fsString(plan.name || ''), stepLabel: fsString(step.content || step.label || ''), triggerId: fsString(step.triggerId || ''), firstName: fsString(c.firstName || ''), lastName: fsString(c.lastName || ''), email: fsString(c.email || ''), phone: fsString(c.phone || ''), mailingAddress: fsString(c.mailingAddress || ''), contactId: fsString(c.uid || ''), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), createdAtMs: fsInt(Date.now()) }; await firestoreWrite(token, 'zapier_events/' + evId, fields); return true; } catch (e) { return false; } } // POST /api/v1/contacts → create a contact (Zapier "Create Contact" action). // Authed by Bearer token (who = resolved agent). Mirrors inbound-lead field handling. async function apiCreateContact(request, env, cors, who) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const _lc = {}; for (const _k in body) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, _k)) _lc[String(_k).trim().toLowerCase()] = body[_k]; } const _pick = (...keys) => { for (const k of keys) { const v = _lc[k.toLowerCase()]; if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== '') return String(v).trim(); } return ''; }; let firstName = _pick('firstName', 'first', 'fname', 'first_name'); let lastName = _pick('lastName', 'last', 'lname', 'last_name'); const _full = _pick('name', 'fullName', 'full_name', 'contactName'); if (!firstName && _full) { const _p = _full.split(/\s+/); firstName = _p.shift() || ''; if (!lastName) lastName = _p.join(' '); } const email = _pick('email', 'emailAddress', 'email_address'); const phone = _pick('phone', 'phoneNumber', 'phone_number', 'mobile', 'tel'); const notes = _pick('notes', 'message', 'comments', 'comment', 'description'); const source = _pick('source', 'leadSource', 'lead_source') || 'Zapier'; const address = _pick('address', 'mailingAddress', 'propertyAddress'); const leadType = _pick('leadType', 'lead_type', 'type'); const leadStatus = _pick('leadStatus', 'status'); const leadSegment = _pick('leadSegment', 'segment'); const smartPlanId = _pick('smartPlanId'); const smartPlanName = _pick('smartPlanName'); if (!firstName && !email && !phone) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'contact needs a name, email, or phone' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); const contactId = 'lead_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7); const fields = { firstName: fsString(firstName), lastName: fsString(lastName), email: fsString(email), phone: fsString(phone), agentId: fsString(who.agentId), role: fsString((leadType === 'seller' || leadType === 'both') ? 'seller' : 'buyer'), journeyType: fsString(leadType === 'both' ? 'both' : ((leadType === 'seller') ? 'seller' : 'buyer')), crmStatus: fsString(['pipeline', 'active', 'nurture', 'cold', 'contact'].indexOf(leadStatus) >= 0 ? leadStatus : 'pipeline'), segment: fsString(leadSegment || ''), currentStep: fsInt(0), currentSellerStep: fsInt(0), isContact: fsBool(true), portalAccess: fsBool(false), source: fsString(source), notes: fsString(notes || ''), mailingAddress: fsString(address || ''), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), lastActive: fsTimestamp() }; if (smartPlanId) { const enrollDay = (typeof spTodayET === 'function') ? spTodayET() : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); fields.smartPlanId = fsString(smartPlanId); fields.smartPlanName = fsString(smartPlanName || 'Plan'); fields.smartPlanOwner = fsString(who.agentId); fields.smartPlanStatus = fsString('active'); fields.smartPlanStep = fsInt(0); fields.smartPlanNextDue = fsString(enrollDay); fields.smartPlanEnrolledAt = fsString(enrollDay); } const fsResp = await firestoreWrite(token, 'users/' + contactId, fields); if (!fsResp.ok) { const t = await fsResp.text(); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'write failed', detail: t }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } // notify the agent (same as inbound-lead) if (env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) { const nm = ((firstName || '') + ' ' + (lastName || '')).trim() || email || 'New Contact'; await pushToAgentId(token, env, who.agentId, ' New Contact (Zapier)', nm + ' added via Zapier', 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?lead=' + contactId); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, id: contactId, agentId: who.agentId }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // ─── Per-tenant email cap: count + gate a tenant's bulk sends (daily + monthly) ─── async function consumeEmailQuota(token, agentId, requested) { requested = Math.max(0, parseInt(requested, 10) || 0); if (!agentId) return { allowed: requested, uncapped: true, dailyCap: EMAIL_DAILY_CAP, monthlyCap: EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP }; // Owner bypass: a user doc flagged emailUnlimited:true is never capped. (Still draws on the shared // Resend plan — "unlimited" means the app won't throttle you, not that the sends are free.) try { const a = await _ntResolveAgent(token, agentId); const doc = (a && a._doc) || {}; if (doc.emailUnlimited === true) return { allowed: requested, uncapped: true, dailyCap: EMAIL_DAILY_CAP, monthlyCap: EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP }; } catch(e){} const today = spTodayET(); // YYYY-MM-DD (America/New_York) const month = today.slice(0, 7); // YYYY-MM const path = 'email_quota/' + agentId; let dayCount = 0, monthCount = 0; try { const cur = await fsGetDoc(token, path); if (cur) { if (cur.day === today) dayCount = parseInt(cur.dayCount, 10) || 0; if (cur.month === month) monthCount = parseInt(cur.monthCount, 10) || 0; } } catch (e) {} const dailyRemaining = Math.max(0, EMAIL_DAILY_CAP - dayCount); const monthlyRemaining = Math.max(0, EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP - monthCount); const allowed = Math.max(0, Math.min(requested, dailyRemaining, monthlyRemaining)); try { await firestoreWrite(token, path, { day: fsString(today), dayCount: fsInt(dayCount + allowed), month: fsString(month), monthCount: fsInt(monthCount + allowed), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch (e) {} return { allowed, requested, dailyRemaining, monthlyRemaining, dailyCap: EMAIL_DAILY_CAP, monthlyCap: EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP }; } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // MLS ALERT EMAIL PIPELINE // An agent points a Matrix saved search at c-@in.nesttrack.io instead of // the client. Everything below turns those emails into listing cards the client // rates in the app, so the agent finally sees what their buyer actually thinks. // No MLS licence, no per-agent paperwork, works in any market on day one. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Bodies arrive quoted-printable. Undo soft line breaks first, then =XX. function _qpDecode(s) { return String(s || '') .replace(/=\r?\n/g, '') .replace(/=([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})/g, function (_, h) { return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)); }); } function _htmlUnescape(s) { return String(s || '') .replace(/ /g, ' ').replace(/•/g, '\u2022') .replace(/&/g, '&').replace(/</g, '<').replace(/>/g, '>') .replace(/"/g, '"').replace(/'/g, "'") .replace(/&#(\d+);/g, function (_, d) { return String.fromCharCode(+d); }); } function _stripTags(s) { return _htmlUnescape(String(s || '').replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ' ')).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); } // The tag name is captured and back-referenced so a

holding nested s // reads through to its own

. Matching the first closing tag of any kind // truncated "4 bd \u2022 3 ba \u2022 2,636 sqft" down to just "4 bd". function _mlsClassRe(cls, flags) { return new RegExp("<(\\w+)[^>]*\\bclass=['\"][^'\"]*\\b" + cls + "\\b[^'\"]*['\"][^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/\\1>", flags); } function _byClass(block, cls) { var m = block.match(_mlsClassRe(cls, 'i')); return m ? _stripTags(m[2]) : ''; } function _allByClass(block, cls) { var re = _mlsClassRe(cls, 'gi'), out = [], m; while ((m = re.exec(block))) out.push(_stripTags(m[2])); return out; } // OneHome tokens are plain base64 JSON: contactid, setid, agentid, listingid. function _decodeOneHomeToken(tok) { try { var s2 = String(tok || '').replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'); s2 += '='.repeat((4 - (s2.length % 4)) % 4); return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(s2)))); } catch (e) { return null; } } // Status is an image; the filename is the reliable signal. Collected from real // samples, with the rest pre-mapped so they work the first time one arrives. var MLS_STATUS_MAP = { new_listing:'New Listing', price_decreased:'Price Decreased', price_increased:'Price Increased', back_on_market:'Back on Market', open_house:'Open House', under_contract:'Under Contract', pending:'Pending', sold:'Sold', closed:'Sold' }; function _mlsStatus(block) { var m = block.match(/assets\/email\/([a-z_]+)\.png/i); if (m && MLS_STATUS_MAP[m[1].toLowerCase()]) { return { status: MLS_STATUS_MAP[m[1].toLowerCase()], statusKey: m[1].toLowerCase() }; } // alt sometimes runs straight into the next attribute: alt="New Listing"height="22" var a = block.match(/assets\/email\/[a-z_]+\.png"[^>]*?alt="([^"]+)"/i) || block.match(/alt="([^"]+)"\s*height="22"/i); if (a) return { status: a[1].trim(), statusKey: a[1].toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_') }; return { status: '', statusKey: '' }; } function _mlsSpecs(line) { var out = { beds: null, baths: null, sqft: null }; var b = line.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*bd/i); if (b) out.beds = parseFloat(b[1]); var ba = line.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*ba/i); if (ba) out.baths = parseFloat(ba[1]); var sf = line.match(/([\d,]+)\s*sqft/i); if (sf) out.sqft = parseInt(sf[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10); return out; } // Walk the MIME parts and return the decoded text/html body. Handles base64 and // quoted-printable, and recurses into nested multiparts — a forwarded email wraps the // original inside another multipart, which is exactly how the first real test failed. function _mimeHtmlPart(raw, depth) { depth = depth || 0; if (depth > 4) return raw; const bMatch = raw.match(/boundary="?([^"\r\n;]+)"?/i); if (!bMatch) return _decodePart(raw, raw); const parts = raw.split('--' + bMatch[1]); let best = ''; for (const part of parts) { if (!/Content-Type:/i.test(part)) continue; if (/Content-Type:\s*multipart\//i.test(part)) { const inner = _mimeHtmlPart(part, depth + 1); if (inner && inner.length > best.length) best = inner; continue; } if (!/Content-Type:\s*text\/html/i.test(part)) continue; const decoded = _decodePart(part, ''); if (decoded.length > best.length) best = decoded; } return best || raw; } function _decodePart(part, fallback) { const split = part.split(/\r?\n\r?\n/); // headers end at the first blank line const headers = split[0] || ''; const bodyText = split.slice(1).join('\n\n'); if (!bodyText) return fallback || ''; if (/Content-Transfer-Encoding:\s*base64/i.test(headers)) { try { const bin = atob(bodyText.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]/g, '')); // atob yields bytes \u2014 reinterpret as UTF-8 so em dashes and accents survive. const bytes = Uint8Array.from(bin, function (c) { return c.charCodeAt(0); }); return new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(bytes); } catch (e) { return bodyText; } } if (/Content-Transfer-Encoding:\s*quoted-printable/i.test(headers)) { return _qpDecode(bodyText); } return bodyText; } function parseMlsAlert(rawBody) { var html = /=\r?\n|=3D/.test(rawBody) ? _qpDecode(rawBody) : rawBody; var ctx = null; var t0 = html.match(/portal\.onehome\.com\/[^"']*[?&]token=([A-Za-z0-9+/=_-]+)/); if (t0) ctx = _decodeOneHomeToken(t0[1]); var parts = html.split(//); var listings = []; for (var i = 1; i < parts.length; i += 2) { var recordId = parts[i], block = parts[i + 1] || ''; if (listings.some(function (l) { return l.recordId === recordId; })) continue; // fence is doubled var specs = _allByClass(block, 'highlight-specs'); var specLine = specs.find(function (x) { return /bd|ba|sqft/i.test(x); }) || ''; var mlsLine = specs.find(function (x) { return /MLS\s*#/i.test(x); }) || ''; var priceTxt = _byClass(block, 'highlight-price'); var row = Object.assign({ recordId: recordId, mlsNumber: (mlsLine.match(/MLS\s*#\s*(\S+)/i) || [])[1] || '', price: priceTxt, priceNum: parseInt(String(priceTxt).replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''), 10) || null, propType: _byClass(block, 'highlight-title'), street: _byClass(block, 'highlight-description'), cityState: _byClass(block, 'highlight-address'), photoUrl: _htmlUnescape((block.match(/src="(https:\/\/[^"]*GetMedia\.ashx[^"]*)"/) || [])[1] || ''), listingUrl: _htmlUnescape((block.match(/href="(https:\/\/portal\.onehome\.com\/[^"]*listing[^"]*)"/) || [])[1] || '') }, _mlsSpecs(specLine), _mlsStatus(block)); row.address = (row.street + (row.cityState ? ', ' + row.cityState : '')).trim(); if (row.mlsNumber || row.street) listings.push(row); } // The welcome email carries no listings and a welcome.jpg banner. It is ALSO the // opt-in: Matrix leaves a saved search inactive until someone clicks through to // OneHome once. There is no link labelled "confirm" \u2014 "View Properties" is it. // Verified: a plain GET on that URL flips the search from pending to sending. var isWelcome = listings.length === 0 && /assets\/email\/welcome\.jpg/i.test(html); var activateUrl = ''; if (isWelcome) { var a2 = html.match(/href="(https:\/\/portal\.onehome\.com\/[^"]*properties\?token=[^"]*)"/); if (a2) activateUrl = _htmlUnescape(a2[1]); } return { contactId: ctx ? ctx.contactid : null, setId: ctx ? ctx.setid : null, agentId: ctx ? ctx.agentid : null, sentTo: ctx ? ctx.email : '', isWelcome: isWelcome, activateUrl: activateUrl, listings: listings }; } // Follow the opt-in link so the search starts delivering. Best-effort, but logged: // a silent failure means the agent set up a search that never sends anything. // Activating a saved search needs a REAL browser. OneHome is a JavaScript app: the code that // registers the visit runs client-side, so a plain fetch returns 200 while Matrix still shows // the search as inactive. That false success cost us most of a morning. // // Cloudflare Browser Rendering runs actual headless Chrome. Using the REST API rather than // @cloudflare/puppeteer on purpose — the npm package needs a bundler, and this worker is // deployed by pasting one file into Quick Edit. // // Requires: Workers Paid, CF_ACCOUNT_ID, and CF_BROWSER_TOKEN (an API token with // Browser Rendering edit permission). Without those it degrades to the in-app Activate // button rather than failing silently. async function _mlsActivate(env, url) { if (!url) return { ok: false, reason: 'no activation url' }; if (!env.CF_ACCOUNT_ID || !env.CF_BROWSER_TOKEN) { return { ok: false, reason: 'browser rendering not configured' }; } try { const r = await fetch( 'https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/' + env.CF_ACCOUNT_ID + '/browser-rendering/content', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.CF_BROWSER_TOKEN, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ url: url, // Wait for the network to settle so the registering script has actually run. gotoOptions: { waitUntil: 'networkidle0', timeout: 30000 } }) } ); const j = await r.json().catch(function () { return null; }); if (!r.ok || !j || j.success !== true) { return { ok: false, reason: 'render ' + r.status + ': ' + (j && j.errors ? JSON.stringify(j.errors).slice(0, 160) : 'no body') }; } // The page rendered. Not absolute proof Matrix recorded it — only a listing alert is — // but this is the same thing a browser does, which is what worked by hand. return { ok: true, bytes: (j.result || '').length }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'render error: ' + (e && e.message || 'network') }; } } // Resolve c-@in.nesttrack.io back to the client. Per-client address rather // than one shared inbox: the To: header only carries the client's real email when // the search still goes to them, and once NestTrack is the sole recipient that // disappears. The token is unambiguous. async function _mlsResolveClient(token, inboundAddr) { // Strip whatever prefix the address carries. The app generates mls-@; the c- // form is from an earlier draft. Accept either so old addresses keep working, and // lowercase it because some mail servers rewrite the local part's case in transit. const tok = String(inboundAddr || '').split('@')[0].replace(/^(mls|c)-/i, '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!tok) return null; try { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'mlsFeedToken' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: tok } } }, limit: 1 }); for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (r.document && r.document.fields) { const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); d.uid = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); return d; } } } catch (e) { console.log('mls resolve:', e && e.message); } return null; } // One doc per listing per client, keyed on the MLS number so the same house // arriving next week updates rather than piling up. Ratings and firstSeenAt // survive an update \u2014 a price change must not wipe what the client already said. async function _mlsStoreListings(token, client, parsed) { let added = 0, updated = 0; for (const l of parsed.listings) { const key = l.mlsNumber || l.recordId; if (!key) continue; const path = 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + key; let prior = null; try { prior = await fsGetDoc(token, path); } catch (e) {} const fields = { mlsNumber: fsString(l.mlsNumber), recordId: fsString(l.recordId), price: fsString(l.price), priceNum: fsInt(l.priceNum || 0), propType: fsString(l.propType), street: fsString(l.street), cityState: fsString(l.cityState), address: fsString(l.address), beds: fsInt(l.beds || 0), baths: fsInt(l.baths || 0), sqft: fsInt(l.sqft || 0), status: fsString(l.status), statusKey: fsString(l.statusKey), photoUrl: fsString(l.photoUrl), listingUrl: fsString(l.listingUrl), lastActivityAt: fsTimestamp(), agentId: fsString(client.agentId || parsed.agentId || ''), setId: fsString(parsed.setId || ''), lastSeenAt: fsTimestamp() }; if (!prior) { fields.firstSeenAt = fsTimestamp(); added++; } else { updated++; // Price moved on a listing they already reacted to \u2014 worth surfacing to the agent. if (prior.priceNum && l.priceNum && prior.priceNum !== l.priceNum) { fields.priorPriceNum = fsInt(prior.priceNum); fields.priceChangedAt = fsTimestamp(); } } try { await fsPatchFields(token, path, fields, Object.keys(fields)); } catch (e) { console.log('mls store:', e && e.message); } } return { added, updated }; } export default { // Inbound MLS alerts. Cloudflare Email Routing hands the message straight here — // free, and it never touches the Resend quota, which is for outbound only. async email(message, env, ctx) { try { const to = String(message.to || ''); const raw = await new Response(message.raw).text(); // Only accept mail that really came from the MLS. Anyone can guess an address. if (!/northeastmatrixmail\.com|mlsmatrix\.com/i.test(raw)) { console.log('mls email: rejected, not from the MLS \u2014', to); return; } // Pull out the text/html part and decode it properly. Splitting on the header line and // assuming quoted-printable was too naive: Gmail re-encodes forwarded mail as base64, // which produced 33k of gibberish and zero listings. Servers do the same in transit. const body = _mimeHtmlPart(raw); const parsed = parseMlsAlert(body); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await _mlsResolveClient(token, to); if (!client) { console.log('mls email: no client for', to, '\u2014 contactid', parsed.contactId); return; } if (parsed.isWelcome) { // Load the opt-in URL in real headless Chrome so the search actually activates. // If Browser Rendering isn't configured, the URL is stored and the client card // shows an Activate button instead — degraded, not broken. const act = await _mlsActivate(env, parsed.activateUrl); console.log('mls email: welcome for', client.uid, act.ok ? ('\u2014 activated in headless Chrome (' + act.bytes + ' bytes)') : ('\u2014 auto-activation unavailable (' + act.reason + '), Activate button shown instead')); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid, { mlsFeedActivateUrl: fsString(act.ok ? '' : (parsed.activateUrl || '')), mlsFeedSetId: fsString(parsed.setId || ''), mlsFeedWelcomeAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['mlsFeedActivateUrl', 'mlsFeedSetId', 'mlsFeedWelcomeAt']); } catch (e) {} return; } if (!parsed.listings.length) { // Neither listings nor a welcome banner. Print a fingerprint of what actually arrived // rather than guessing \u2014 the three counts below separate "empty digest" from // "template changed" from "we sliced the wrong MIME part". const _subj = (raw.match(/^Subject:\s*(.+)$/mi) || [])[1] || '(none)'; console.log('mls email: nothing to store for', client.uid, '| subject:', _subj.trim().slice(0, 60), '| raw:', raw.length, '| body:', body.length, '| @Record fences:', (body.match(/@Record:/g) || []).length, '| welcome.jpg:', /assets\/email\/welcome\.jpg/i.test(body), '| qp-decoded welcome.jpg:', /assets\/email\/welcome\.jpg/i.test(_qpDecode(body)), '| highlight-price:', (body.match(/highlight-price/g) || []).length, // If the OneHome links survive but the classes and comments do not, the sender // stripped the markup \u2014 Gmail does this when forwarding. Real MLS sends arrive intact. '| onehome links:', (body.match(/portal\.onehome\.com/g) || []).length, '| any class attr:', /class=/i.test(body), '| any html comment:', /
